aṇṇā talaimaiyil tirāviṭa muṉṉēṟṟak kaḻakam' uruvāṉatu. ik cuṭci, nammiṭamiruntu pirintu ceṉṟa āntiramum, kēraḷamum moḻi vaḻiyē amaintu aḻaikkappaṭumpōtu 'tamiḻakam" eṉa aḻaikkap peṟāmal ceṉṉai mākāṇam' eṉa aḻaikkappaṭuvatai etirttup pōrāṭiyatu. kiḷarccikkup piṉ akattīcuvaram, tōvāḷai, kalkuḷam, viḷavaṅkōṭu, ceṅkōṭṭaiyiṉ perum pakutiyum ceṉṉaiyuṭaṉ iṇaikkappeṟṟut tamiḻ mānilamāṉatu. 
1957-l naṭanta viṭutalai intiyāvil iraṇṭāvatu potut tērtalilum pērāyak kaṭciyē veṟṟi peṟṟatu. kēraḷamum, āntiramum taṉi mānilaṅkaḷāṉapiṉ tamiḻakam ciṟuttuviṭṭatu. itaṉāl, amaic caravaiyum curuṅkiviṭṭatu. itil.ku. kāmarācar mutalamaiccarākavum, em. paktavaccalam, ci. cuppiramaṇiyam mutaliya eḻuvar amaiccarkaḷākavum patavi ēṟṟaṉar. tirāviṭa muṉṉēṟṟak kaḻakam toṭarntu intiyaiyetirt tum, ceṉṉai mānilattait 'tamiḻnāṭu' eṉṟu māṟṟumpaṭiyum pōrāṭṭaṅkaḷ naṭattiyatu. 1957l ceṉṉai mānilam tamiḻnāṭu' eṉṟu aṟiyappaṭum eṉṟum, āṅkilattil "ceṉṉai' eṉak kuṟippiṭavēṇṭum meṉṟum kāmarācar amaiccaravai tīrmāṉittatu. paḷḷi iṟutip paṭippuvarai ilavacak kalviyum, ēḻai māṇavarukku matiya uṇavuttiṭṭa, mum koṇṭu vantu kāmarācar tamiḻariṉ uḷḷak kōyilil iṭam peṟṟār. 
1967-l naṭanta potut tērtalilum pērāyak kaṭciyē veṟṟi peṟṟatu. kāmarācarē ceṉṉai mānila mutalvar āṉār. em. pakta vaccalam, ci. cuppiramaṇiyam mutaliya eḻuvar amaiccaravaiyil iṭam peṟṟaṉar. 1955-l cēlam, tarumapuri eṉa iru māvaṭṭaṅkaḷākap pirikkap peṟṟatu. cuṉṉiyākumari māvaṭṭamum tōṉṟiyatu. kocciyiliruntu tiruvaṉantapuram pirikkappeṟṟatu. 1963-l kāmarācar mūtta uṟuppiṉar, cuṭcikkup paṇiyāṟṟap patavi tuṟakkum taṉ kōṭpāṭṭiṉpaṭi tāmē patavi tuṟantār. ākavē, 1963 mutal 16 varai em. paktavaccalam mutalamaiccar āṉār, taṉatu tirucci mānāṭṭu muṭiviṉpaṭi it tērtalil īṭupaṭṭatirāviṭa muṉṉēṟṟakkaḻakam oru cila iṭaṅkaḷaip piṭittatu. 
1967-l naṭanta potut tērtalil tirāviṭa muṉṉēṟṟak kaḻakam perumpāṉmaiyāṉa iṭaṅkaḷaip peṟṟu aṟiñar aṇṇā talaimai yil amaiccaravai amaittatu. irā. neṭuñceḻiyaṉ, mu. karuṇāniti mutaliya oṉpatu amaiccarkaḷ itil iṭam peṟṟaṉar, aṟiñar aṇṇāviṉ talaimaiyilāṉa tirāviṭa muṉṟēṟṟak kaḻakattiṉ inta amaiccaravai makkaḷ, kaṭcikaḷ, atikārikaḷiṉ ottuḻaippaik kōrip peṟṟup pala putiya cīrtiruttaṅkaḷaik koṇṭuvantatu. ceṉṉai mākāṇam tamiḻnāṭu' eṉṟu māṟṟappaṭum caṭṭattaiyum, intiyai aṭiyōṭu nīkkit tamiḻē mānilam muḻuvatum kaṟkum kaṟpikkum āḷum 
kipi 19, 20 ām nāṟṟāṇṭukaḷil tamiḻakam 
203 
moḻiyākavum, āṅkilam tuṇai moḻiyākavum irukkum caṭṭattai yum koṇṭu vantatu. 1957-l ceṉṉai mākāṇam 'tamiḻnāṭu' eṉa aṟiyappaṭum tīrmāṉattaik kāmarācar amaiccaravaik koṇṭuvantatu. āṉālum 1967-l aṇṇā amaiccaravaitāṉ caṭṭappaṭi tamiḻnāṭu eṉṟu māṟṟiyatu. 1958 mutalē tamiḻ āṭcimoḻiyākkappaṭṭatāyi ṉum aṇṇāviṉ amaiccaravaiyē caṭṭappaṭi ataṟku mukāmai iṭattaiyum virivākkattaiyum aḷittatu. inti aṭiyōṭu akaṟṟap paṭṭu, inti āciriyarkaḷ vēṟu paṇikaḷil amarttap peṟṟaṉar. 1963 54 ām āṇṭu mutalē palkalaikkaḻakap paṭṭappaṭippil cila pāṭaṅkaḷ tamiḻil kaṟṟukkoṭukkap peṟṟaṉa. āyiṉum, itu caṭṭappaṭiyum, paṭippaṭiyākavum koṇṭuvarappaṭṭu iṉṟu kallūrikaḷil tamiḻ pāṭamoḻiyākiviṭṭatu. intuc camaya vitikaḷiṉpaṭi ceyyappaṭṭa tirumaṇaṅkaḷ tavira piṟa tirumaṇaṅkaḷ cellupaṭiyākāveṉṟa nilaimai māṟiperiyāriṉ taṉmāṉa (cuyamariyātait tirumaṇaṅkaḷum cellupaṭi yākum caṭṭam koṇṭu varappaṭṭatu. tāḻttappaṭṭa āṇ allatu peṇ vēṟu kulattavaruṭaṉ maṇa uṟavu koṇṭāl ataṉai uṉakkuvic kum poruṭṭut taṅkap patakkam vaḻaṅkum tiṭṭamum koṇṭu varap paṭṭatu. kumukāyac cīrtiruttaṅkaḷaiyē taṉ mutukantaṇṭāykkoṇṭa tirāviṭa muṉṉēṟṟak kaḻakam taṉ amaiccaravaiyilēyē ittakaiya cīrtiruttac caṭṭaṅkaḷaiyum, tiṭṭaṅkaḷaiyum koṇṭu vantu veṟṟi peṟṟatu. 3-2-1969-l aṟiñar aṇṇā kālamāṉār: mu. karuṇāniti mutalamaiccar āṉār. 
1971-l naṭanta iṭaittērtalilum tirāviṭa muṉṉēṟṟak kaḻakamē veṟṟippeṟṟatu. aṇṇāvukkup piṟaku eṭṭu nāḷkaḷ irā. neṭuñ ceḻiyaṉ taṟkālika mutalvarāka iruntār. avarukkup piṉṉum, iṭait tērtalukkup piṉṉum kalaiñar mu. karuṇānitiyē toṭarntu muta lamaiccarāka iruntār. 10-2-1959 mutal 31-1-197varai irunta karuṇāniti yiṉ amaiccaravai piṟa mānilaṅkaḷum piṉpaṟṟumāṟu pala ariya tiṭṭaṅkaḷai vakuttuc ceyalpaṭutti vantatu. kalvi, poruḷiyal, kumukāyac cīrtiruttaṅkaḷil tamiḻakam iṉṟu mutaliṭam peṟṟu viḷaṅkiya pēruntu vaṇṭikaḷai nāṭṭuṭaimaiyākkiyatu; kappal vāṇikattai mēṟkoṇṭatu; vāṉvaḻip pōkkuvarattai mēṟkoḷḷaviruntatu; mānilat tiṭṭakkuḻuvai ēṟpaṭutti ataṉ karuttukaḷai ēṟṟu naṭappatu: pacumaip puraṭci, veḷḷaip puraṭci, uṇavup puraṭci mutaliyaṉavum, taṉi vāriyaṅkaḷai amaittup poruḷiyal vaḷarcciyait tūṇṭuvatumāṉa pala ariya tiṭṭaṅkaḷ tamiḻnāṭṭil poruḷiyal vaḷarccikku ēṟṟam tarukiṉṟaṉa. kāviri nīrp paṅkīṭu muṟaiyum tīrntu, nīrppācaṉat tiṭṭaṅ kaḷum, vēḷāṇmait tiṭṭaṅkaḷum, toḻil tiṭṭaṅkaḷum niṟaivēṟi ṉāl tamiḻakam poruḷiyal nilaiyil taṉṉiṟaivu peṟuvatōṭu piṟa mānilaṅkaḷukkum utavum nilaiyil amaiyum.

tāynila varalāṟu 
kalviyilum, kumukāya muṉṉēṟṟattilum tamiḻnāṭu mutaliṭam peṟṟatu. kaṇṇoḷi vaḻaṅkum tiṭṭam, muṭavar, kuruṭar, iravar (piccaikkārar maṟuvāḻvut tiṭṭam,ētili aṉātaikaḷukku vāḻvaḷit kum tiṭṭam mutaliyaṉa karuṇānitiyiṉ ciṟanta tiṭṭaṅkaḷākum. nāṭu muḻuvatum toḻunōyāḷarkaḷukku viṭutikaḷ amaikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. irappatum (piccai eṭuppatum, māntaṉai māntaṉ kai vaṇṭiyil vaittu iḻuttuccelvatum caṭṭaṅkaḷāl kuṟṟaṅkaḷākkappaṭṭaṉa. naravaṇṭi (kai rikcāvukkuppatil cumai mitivaṇṭi caikkiḷ rikcā) koṭukkappaṭṭatu. narikkuṟavar pōṉṟa nāṭaṟṟa ētilikaḷukkum taṉiyē kuṭiyiruppukaḷum, kalvi, toḻiṟkūṭaṅkaḷum amaikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. kuṭicaimāṟṟu vāriyam, tāḻttappaṭṭōr vīṭṭu ēntu vāriyam mutaliyaṉavum pūcāriyar aruccakar) caṭṭavaraivum kumukāya nalaṉukkākak koṇṭu varappaṭṭa ciṟanta tiṭṭaṅkaḷākum, piṟpaṭṭōr nalattuṟai 
nayaṉmaik kaṭci 1920 ām āṇṭu amaiccaravaiyai amaittati liruntē piṟpaṭṭōr nalattiṟkē pāṭupaṭa muṉaintatu. ataṉ maṟupiṟavi yāṉa tirāviṭa muṉṉēṟṟak kaḻakam piṟpaṭṭōr nalattuṟai oṉṟait taṉiyē ēṟpaṭuttip piṟpaṭṭōrukku aracup paṇikaḷilum, kalvik kūṭaṅkaḷilum atika iṭaṅkaḷai otukkiyuḷḷatu. nāṭu muḻuvatum piṟpaṭṭōr kuḻantaikaḷukkāṉa viṭutikaḷ amaikkappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa, tāḻttappaṭṭōr nalattuṟaiyuṭaṉ iṇaintu ceyalpaṭṭa it tuṟaiyait taṉiyē pirittu ataṟku taṉiyē amaiccaraiyum amarttic ceyalpaṭṭa perumai mu. karuṇānitiyaiyē cārum. 
itaṉāl, tāḻttappaṭṭōr-piṟpaṭṭōr calukaip pōrāṭ ṭaṅkaḷum, cāti uṇarvukaḷum vaḷarntu varuvatait taṭukka muṭiyavillai, itaṉaip pōkkak kumukāyac cīrtiruttattaiyē mūccākakkoṇṭa tirāviṭa muṉṉēṟṟak kaḻakam cātiyaṭippaṭaiyai oḻittupporuḷiyal aṭippaṭaiyil calukaikaḷ vaḻaṅkiṉāl kumukāyattil cāti uṇarvu vaḷarāmal taṭukkalām eṉa aṟiñar palarum karutukiṉṟaṉar. tāḻttappaṭ ṭōr māṇavar viṭutikaḷum, piṟpaṭṭōr māṇavar viṭutikaḷum cāti uṇarvukaḷai uruvākkum vitaip paṇṇaikaḷākiviṭṭaṉa. aracu itaṉait takkapaṭi āyntu ceyalpaṭa vēṇṭum. māṇavamaṇikaḷ maṉam tiruntic cātiuṇarvaiviṭṭoḻittu muṉṉēṟa vēṇṭum. 
iṉṟu tamiḻakattil mottam 30 māvaṭṭaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. avai ceṉṉai, tiruvaḷḷūr, kāñcipuram, tiruvaṇṇāmalai, vēlūr, viḻuppuram, kaṭalūr, cēlam, nāmakkal, tarmapuri, kōyamputtūr, īrōṭu, nīlamalai, tirucci, karūr, ariyalūr, tañcāvūr, tiruvārūr, nākai, maturai, tēṉi, tiṇṭukkal, irāmanātapuram, civakaṅkai, virutunakar, tirunelvēli, tūttukkuṭi, kaṉṉiyākumari, putukkōṭṭai ākiyavaiyām. 1974 ām

āṇṭu poṅkaṟ putu nāḷaṉṟu putukkōṭṭai māvaṭṭam amaikkap peṟṟatu. itil putukkōṭṭai, aṟantāṅki, kuḷattūr, tirumaiyam, ālaṅkuṭi ākiya vaṭṭaṅkaḷ aṭaṅkiyuḷḷaṉa. pattu ūrāṭci oṉṟiyaṅ kaḷum, 490 ūrāṭcikaḷum, oṉpatu pērūrāṭcikaḷum, oru nakarāṭciyum im māvaṭṭattil uḷḷaṉa. itaṉ piṉṉarē pala māvaṭṭaṅkaḷ iraṇṭākavum mūṉṟākavum pirikkappaṭṭaṉa. ūrāṭcittuṟai vaḷarcci 
uḷḷāṭcimuṟai stala stāpaṉa āṭci eṉṟavuṭaṉ nām rippaṉ pirapu (1880-1884) eṉpāṉai niṉaivu kūrkiṟōm. āṉāl, kipi. 1871-l ēṟpaṭṭa uḷḷāṭcic caṭṭappaṭi (Local Fund Act) ceṉṉai māvaṭṭattil māvaṭṭak kaḻakaṅkaḷum (District Bards), vaṭṭak kaḻakaṅ kaḷum (Taluk Boards), karāṭci oṉṟiyaṅkaḷum (Union Boards) 
ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. māvaṭṭak kaḻakaṅkaḷ 
1871 ām āṇṭuc caṭṭappaṭi ceṉṉai maṇṭilattil māvaṭṭac kaḻakaṅkaḷ ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. ik kaḻakaṅkaḷ nilavari varuvāyttuṟaiyiṉ kīḻc ceyalpaṭṭaṉa. eṉavē, ovvoru varuvāyt tuṟaip piriviṉ kīḻum oṉṟu allatu iraṇṭu kaḻakaṅkaḷ niṟuvappaṭṭaṉa. kōṭṭa varuvāyt tuṟai atikāri kalekṭar allatu kōṭṭāṭcit talaivar) itaṉ talaivarāṉār. itil aracāl amarttappaṭṭa iraṇṭu allatu mūṉṟu uṟuppiṉar iṭam peṟṟaṉar. inta uṟuppiṉar conta nilam allatu cottuṭaiyavarkaḷāyiruppar. 1884-l koṇṭuvarappaṭṭa ceṉṉai uḷḷāṭcic caṭṭam' ovvoru māvaṭṭattilum māvaṭṭakkaḻakam ēṟpaṭavum, atil 24 uṟuppiṉarkaḷ iṭam peṟavum vakai ceytatu. inta uṟuppiṉarkaḷil oru cilar āḷunarāl amarttappaṭṭaṉar. oru cilar vaṭṭa ūrāṭci oṉṟiyak kaḻakaṅkaḷālum vari koṭuppōrālum tērn teṭukkappaṭṭaṉar. oru cila varikaḷai araciṉ opputal peṟṟu vitikkum atikārattai ik kaḻakaṅkaḷ peṟṟaṉa. vaṭṭak kaḻakaṅkaḷ (Taluk Boards) 
māvaṭṭak kaḻakaṅkaḷukkukkīḻ vaṭṭak kaḻakaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa, ovvoru vaṭṭak kaḻakattilum aracāl amarttappaṭṭum, varikoṭup pōrālum, oṉṟiyakkaḻakattālum tērnteṭukkappaṭṭum paṉṉiraṇṭu uṟuppiṉar iṭam peṟṟaṉar. māvaṭṭa nilavarittuṟai atikāri itaṉ talaivar āṉār. māvaṭṭak kaḻakam taṉ muṭivukkēṟpa vaṭṭak kaḻakaṅ kaḷukkup paṇam otukkīṭu ceytatu. varumāṉattiṟkēṟpa māvaṭṭa, vaṭṭakkaḻakaṅkaḷukkuk kuṟippiṭṭa viḻukkāṭṭut tokaiyaiyum utavit tokaiyaiyum aracu otukkiyatu.

tāynilavaralāṟu 
oṉṟiyak kaḻakaṅkaḷ (Union Board) 
pala ūrkaḷ oṉṟiṇaikkappaṭṭu oṉṟiyamākkappaṭṭu oṉṟiyak kaḻakattāl āḷappaṭṭatu. ovvōr ūriṉ talaivarum itil uṟuppiṉ rāyiruppār. kuṟaintatu aintu pēr itil uṟuppiṉarāka irukka vēṇṭum. itil uṟuppāṇmai peṟṟa ōr ūrttalaivarē oṉṟiyattiṉ talaivar rāka (Union Chairman) iruppār. ūrttalaivarkaḷait tavirtta maṟṟa uṟuppiṉarkaḷ āḷunarālum, varikoṭuppōrālum amarttappaṭuvar. 
vīṭṭu variyē oṉṟiyattiṉ mukāmaivaruvāyākum. itaittavira vaṭṭak kaḻakamum oru tokaiyai aḷikkum. taṉakkuk kīḻuḷḷa ūrkaḷiṉ potuc cālaikaḷ, kuṭinīr, tuppuravu, nilaviyal mutaliyavaṟṟai oṉṟiyam 
kavaṉittatu. ūravaik kaḻakaṅkaḷ 
1920 ām āṇṭu ravai (pañcāyattuc caṭṭappaṭi ūrtōṟum paravai pañcāyattu ēṟpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. ovvōr ūravaiyilum ēḻu mutal patiṉaintukku uṭpaṭṭa uṟuppiṉar, avvūril 25 akavaikku mēṟpaṭṭa āṭavarāl tērnteṭukkappaṭṭu iṭampeṟṟaṉar. cottu allatu nilam uḷḷavar maṭṭumē varikoṭuppōr oppōlaiyurimai uṭaiyavar eṉṟu irunta nilaimāṟi 25 akavai nirampiya āṭavar oppōlaiyurimai peṟṟu vaḷaravai uṟuppiṉarait tērnteṭuttaṉar. iccaṭṭattiṉāl nilamillāta ēḻai makkaḷukku, kuṟippākat tāḻttap paṭṭa makkaḷukkum ippōvai urimai aḷikkappaṭṭatu. naṉarukku vēṇṭiya ellāc ceyalkaḷaiyum ivvūralaiyē ceytu koṇṭatu. vīṭṭuvari, toḻilvari, mutaliyaṉavum, potuccottukaḷiliruntu varum varumāṉaṅkaḷukkum itaṟkum varuvāyāyiṉa. mānilam muḻuvatilumuḷḷa ūravaikaḷaik kaṇkāṇikka mānila uṇaravaip pativāḷar oruvar amarttappaṭṭār. ivarē maṟṟa ūrāṭcik kaḻakaṅkaḷaiyum, nakarāṭci maṉṟaṅkaḷaiyum kaṇkāṇittār. 
mēṟkūṟiya ūrāṭcik kaḻakaṅkaḷ nāḷāvaṭṭattil eṇṇikkai yil atikarittatōṭu, avvappoḻutu koṇṭuvarappaṭṭa cīrtiruttac caṭṭaṅkaḷāl pala māṟṟaṅkaḷaip peṟṟaṉa. 127-l mānila aravaip pativāḷarē nēraṭiyāka ūravaikaḷukku utavittokai vaḻaṅkum atikāram peṟṟār. tokaiyum atikarittatu. paḷḷikkūṭaṅkaḷai ūrāṭcik kaḻakaṅkaḷē ēṟṟu naṭattum urimaiyum vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatu. māvaṭṭak kaḻakaṅkaḷiṉ uṟuppiṉar eṇṇikkaiyum uyarntatu. kōṭṭāṭcit talaivarāyirunta nilaimāṟi āḷunarāl amarttap paṭṭavar talaivar āṉār. varavu celavuttiṭṭattait tāmē vakuttuk koḷḷum urimaiyaiyum ik kaḻakam peṟṟatu. 1930 ām āṇṭiliruntu oṉṟiyaṅkaḷ cuvaikkappaṭṭu paravaikaḷil cērkkappaṭṭaṉa. ākavē, ūrāṭcittuṟaiyiṉ kaṭaici allatu toṭakka nilaiyāka karavaikaḷ

atikāraṅkaḷil aracukkut talaiyiṭum urimaiyuṇṭu. nakarāṇmaik kaḻakam 
1871 ām āṇṭiṉ nakaravirivākkac caṭṭappaṭi tērtal āṇai yāḷar' amarttappaṭṭār. 1878-79-l mānilattiluḷḷa ellā nakaraṅkaḷi lum nakarāṇmaik kaḻakaṅkaḷai ēṟpaṭutta vaḻi ceyyum caṭṭaṅkaḷ koṇṭuvarappaṭṭaṉa. 1884l ceṉṉai nakarāṇmaik kaḻakac caṭṭam" koṇṭu varappaṭṭatu. itaṉpaṭi nakarāṇmaik kaḻaka uṟuppiṉaril mūṉṟil iru pakutiyiṉar tērnteṭukkappaṭṭaṉar. 1920l uṟuppiṉariṉ eṇṇikkai 12 iliruntu 16 āka uyarttappaṭṭatu. 1930 ām āṇṭil peṇkaḷum uṟuppiṉarākum urimai vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatu. talaivarai uṟuppiṉarē tērnteṭuttaṉar. it talaivarkaḷai nīkkum atikāramum uṟuppiṉarukkē vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatu. 
uḷḷāṭci muṟai vaḷarcci ceṉṉai nakaramaṉṟat tōṟṟamum vaḷarcciyum 
ki.pi. 1640]-l ceṉṉaiyil puṉita cārcu kōṭṭaiyaikkaṭṭi ataṉaic cuṟṟilum āṅkilēyarkaḷ kuṭiyamarntaṉar. ki.pi. 1687-l veḷiyāṉa paṭṭaiyac caṭṭappaṭi ceṉṉai nakaraip parāmarikka nakarat tantai (Mayor) amarttappaṭṭār. avarukku utaviyāka 12 nakaramutuvarkaḷ (Aldernet) ti kuṭimakaṉkaḷ ākiyōr amarttappaṭṭaṉar. nakarat tantaiyiṉ patavikkālam ōrāṇṭu eṉṟum nakaramutuvarkaḷ (Aldernmen) patavikkālam āyuṭkālameṉṟum kuṟippiṭappaṭṭatu. piṉṉar nakarat tantaiyai niyamaṉam ceyyāmal nakaramutuvarkaḷē (Aldernmen) tērn teṭuttaṉar. nakaramutuvarkaḷum kuṭimakkaḷāl tērnteṭukkappaṭṭaṉar. kuṭimakkaḷ eṉpavar ceṉṉaivācikaḷāl tērnteṭukkappaṭṭavar.

aṭutta kaṭṭa vaḷarcci rippaṉ pirapu' (kipi. 1880-1888) intiyāviṉ talaimai āḷunarāyiruntapōtu avar koṇṭuvanta uḷḷāṭci muṟait tīrmāṉattiliruntu toṭaṅkiyatu. * 'uḷḷāṭci muṟaiyeṉpatu potu makkaḷukku araciyalil paṅkēṟkak koṭukkum aṭippaṭaip payiṟci yākum" eṉpatu avaruṭaiya kōṭpāṭākum. 
iraṇṭāvatu kaṭṭa vaḷarcciyāka aracu āṇaikkuḻuviṉ paṉmukappaṭuttum parinturaiyākum (Report of the Royal pōpāission for Decent rialization), itaṉāl mānakara maṉṟam, nakara maṉṟam ākiyavai ēṟpaṭṭaṉa, ciṟu nakaraṅkaḷilum nakarāṇmaik kaḻakaṅkaḷ ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. ivaṟṟiṟku aracāṅkamē niti utaviyaḷittatu. ataik koṇṭu kuṭinīrk kuḻāykaḷ, kaḻivunīrk kālvāykaḷ mutaliyaṉa amaikkappaṭṭaṉa. 
mūṉṟāvatu kaṭṭamāka kipi, 1919 ām āṇṭu intiya araciyal caṭṭak kūṟupāṭukaḷ ākum. ivvāṇṭiliruntutāṉ uḷḷāṭcikkāṉa palatiṟappaṭṭa uḷḷāṭcic caṭṭaṅkaḷ (Local Government Acts) veḷivarattoṭaṅkiṉa. 
nāṉkāvatu kaṭṭa vaḷarcci, intiyar viṭutalaiyaṭainta kipi.1947 liruntu toṭaṅkiyatu. 1950-l ceyalpaṭuttappaṭṭa araciyal caṭṭattil uḷḷāṭcimuṟai iṭam peṟṟatu. intiya araciyal caṭṭaviti 40-l "ūrāṭci maṉṟaṅkaḷtāṉ intiya aracāṅkattiṉ aṭippaṭaik kaṭṭamaippākak koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa"'. cepṭampar, 1954-l intiyak kuṭiyaracut talaivar naṭuvaṇ uḷḷāṭcik kaḻakattait toṭaṅkivaittār.

tāynila varalāṟu 
talaimaiyilēṟpaṭṭakkuḻu (Balavantray Metha orumittee) kumukāy vaḷarccit tiṭṭaṅkaḷaiyum makkaḷāṭci muṟaiyil oṉṟiṇaittu, paṉmukappaṭuttic ceyalpaṭa vēṇṭumeṉṟatu. itaṉaṭippaṭaiyiltāṉ, 
(1) ūrkaḷil pañcāyattukkaḷum, - 
(2) ōṉarkaḷai uṭkoṇṭa pakutikaḷil pañcāyattu oṉṟiyaṅ kaḷum, 
(3) māvaṭṭa aḷavil māvaṭṭak kaḻakaṅkaḷum āka, ""mūṉṟaṭukku uḷḷāṭcimuṟai" ēṟpaṭa vaḻivakuttatu. 
ivvāṟu, paṭippaṭiyāka vaḷarccip peṟṟa uḷḷāṭcimuṟai iṉṟu tamiḻakattil makkaḷiṉ aṉṟāṭa uyirt tuṭippākak kāṭci yaḷikkiṟatu. kuṟippākac ceṉṉai maṇṭilattil iraṭṭai āṭci naṭanta kālakkaṭṭattil (ki.pi. 1920-1937) tāṉ itu āḻamāka vērūṉṟat toṭaṅkiyatu. eṭuttukkāṭṭāka:- ceṉṉai uḷḷāṭci caṭṭam, ceṉṉai ūrāṭcikaḷiṉ caṭṭam mutaliyaṉa 1920-l niṟaivēṟṟap paṭṭaṉa. 1930 ām āṇṭu māvaṭṭa nakaramaṉṟaṅkaḷiṉ caṭṭamum, uḷḷāṭcic caṭṭat tiruttamum koṇṭu varappaṭṭaṉa. 1935lēṟpaṭṭa intiya araciyal caṭṭam mānilat taṉṉāṭci muṟaiyai aṟimukap paṭuttiyatu. eṉavētāṉ ivvāṇṭiliruntu toṭaṅkum uḷḷāṭci muṟaiyait ''tamiḻakattilēṟpaṭṭa uḷḷāṭci muṟaiyiṉ poṟkālat toṭarcci eṉpar. 
kipi.1909-tilēṟpaṭṭa intiya araciyal caṭṭappaṭi iculāmiyar, intiyak kiṟittuvar, āṅkilō intiyar mutaliyōrukku iṭam otukkappaṭṭatu. itil cariyāṉa vikitattil iṭam peṟātavarukku ''niyamaṉamuṟai"ppaṭi iṭam otukkappaṭṭatu. āṉāl, 1920lkoṇṭu varappaṭṭa ceṉṉai maṇṭil māvaṭṭa nakaramaṉṟac caṭṭappaṭi inta niyamaṉamuṟai nīkkappaṭṭatu. 1933-l ēṟpaṭṭa caṭṭappaṭi, nakaramaṉṟa āṇaiyar muḻunēra atikāriyāka amarttappaṭṭu uḷḷāṭcici-- ceyalkaḷai avarē naṭaimuṟaippaṭuttiṉār. viṭutalaikkuppiṉ nakarmaṉṟat talaivarkaḷum, āṇaiyarkaḷum iṇaintu ceyalpaṭṭaṉar, palvēṟu kuḻukkaḷākap pirintu uṟuppiṉarkaḷ ceyalpaṭṭaṉar. aintāṇṭut tiṭṭaṅkaḷum uḷḷāṭci vaḷarcciyum 
aṭittaḷa makkaḷiliruntu, uyarnilaiyiluḷḷa makkaḷ varaiyilāṉa nāṭṭiṉ oṭṭumotta vaḷarccikkākavē, nīṇṭakāla aintāṇṭut tiṭṭaṅkaḷ tayār ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. aintāṇṭut tiṭṭam 1951-l toṭaṅkiyatu. aintāṇṭut tiṭṭaṅkaḷāl ūrāṭci maṉṟaṅkaḷil oṭṭumotta vaḷarcci ēṟpaṭṭatu. ataṟkākavē, putiya pañcāyattuc caṭṭaṅkaḷ koṇṭuvarappaṭṭatōṭu naṭaimuṟaiyilirunta caṭṭaṅ kaḷum tirutti amaikkappaṭṭaṉa. ovvoru pañcāyattiṟkum vēṇṭiya

āṭciyuṭaṉ iṇaintu ceyalpaṭṭatu. 
1951 ām āṇṭu makkaḷ tokaik kaṇakkuppaṭi, intiyāvil 85 viḻukkāṭu makkaḷ karppuṟaṅkaḷil tāṉiruntaṉar. eṉavētāṉ, 1952l kumukāya vaḷarccit tiṭṭamum, vaṭṭāra vaḷarccittiṭṭamum koṇṭu varappaṭṭaṉa. 
vaṭṭāra vaḷarccittuṟai atikārikaḷum, kirāma cēvakarkaḷum piṟarum amarttappaṭṭaṉar. kirāma cukātāra nilaiyaṅkaḷ, kālnaṭai cukātāra nilaiyaṅkaḷ mutaliyaṉa ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. kalvi vaḷarcci mika uyarntatu. vēḷāṇmai vaḷarcci, ciṟutoḻil vaḷarcci mutaliya poru ḷātāra muṉṉēṟṟamum vaḷarntaṉa. ivvāṟu mutal, iraṇṭu, mūṉṟāvatu aintāṇṭut tiṭṭaṅkaḷilēyē muṟaiyē 1951 - 55, 1956-5, 1956 ākiya kālakaṭṭaṅkaḷukkuḷ kirāmap pañcāyattukkaḷ muḻuvaḷarcci aṭaintu viṭṭaṉa. 
nāṉkāvatu: intāvatu aintu āṇṭut tiṭṭakālattil (1889-197) aintāṇṭut tiṭṭaṅkaḷukkuppatilāka ōrāṇṭu tiṭṭaṅkaḷai (Annual Plan) vakuttuc ceyalpaṭutta aracu muṟpaṭṭatu. itil pañcāyattu rācciyam (Panchayat Raj) eṉṟa peyaril uḷḷāṭci muṟaiceyalpaṭṭatu. itil ūrāṭcikaḷai oṉṟiṇaittu māvaṭṭa, vaṭṭāra vaḷarccikku ēṟpa tiṭṭaṅkaḷ ceyalpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. itaṉāl ovvoru vaḷarukkum tēvaippaṭum kuṟainta pakka ''taṉṉiṟaivu' pūrtti ceyyappaṭṭatu. 
1980-85-l āṟāvatu aintāṇṭut tiṭṭam toṭaṅkiyatu. ic kālakkaṭṭattil kumukāyap poruḷātāra mēmpāṭṭil atikak kavaṉam celuttappaṭṭatu. itaṟkākap pañcāyattuc caṭṭaṅkaḷ, uḷḷāṭci muṟai amaippukaḷ mutaliyaṉa māṟṟi amaikkap peṟṟaṉa. mēlum makkaḷāl, makkaḷukkāka naṭattappaṭum āṭciyāka uḷḷāṭci muṟai muṟṟilum māṟṟappaṭṭatu. ' ivvāṟu, aintu āṇṭut tiṭṭakkālaṅkaḷil uḷḷāṭciyaik kaṭṭamaikkattoṭaṅki, ōr āṇṭukāla tiṭṭakkālak kaṭṭamaippāka vum ceyalpaṭṭa uḷḷāṭci muṟai vaḷarcci kālantōṟum āṇṭa araciyal kaṭcikaḷāl valuppeṟṟut tēciyattōṭu iṇaintu oru mukamāyc ceyalpaṭṭatu. ki.pi. 1850-liruntu ceṉṉai mānakarāṭciyiṉ vaḷarcci 
ceṉṉai nakaramaṉṟattiṉ tōṟṟamum vaḷarcciyum paṟṟip pārt tōm. tamiḻakattil uḷḷāṭci muṟai mutalil ceṉṉai nakarattiltāṉ tōṉṟiyateṉpataṉaippārttōm. kipi.1540liruntu 1850 varaiyilāṉa ceṉṉai mānakara vaḷarcciyaiyum pārttōm. tamiḻakattil iraṭṭai āṭci toṭaṅkiya poḻututāṉ uḷḷāṭci maṉṟaṅkaḷiṉ vaḷarcci muṭukkamāka vaḷarntatu. uḷḷāṭci maṉṟac caṭṭaṅkaḷ palavum caṭṭa 
21) 
tāynilavaralāṟu 
maṉṟattāl koṇṭu varappaṭṭaṉa, kiḻakkintiya vaṇika āṭcikkuḻu uḷḷāṭci vaḷarcciyil akkaraik kāṭṭāmaliruntatāl makkaḷāṭcit toṭakkappaṭṭa iraṭṭai āṭcik kālattil muṭukkamāy vaḷarat toṭaṅkiyatu. 1920-lēyē 70-liruntu 80 varaiyilāṉa nakara maṉṟaṅkaḷ tōṉṟiṉa. āyiṉum, 1979 ām āṇṭu intiya araciyal caṭṭattiṟkuppiṉ tāṉ ceṉṉai mānakarāṭci muḻumaiyāka vaḷarat toṭaṅkiyatu. nakaramaṉṟakkuḻu, nilaikkuḻu āṇaiyar, nakarattantai ākiyōr ēṟpaṭṭa ṉar. avarkaḷiṉ atikāraṅkaḷum varaiyaṟukkappaṭṭaṉa. 
nayaṉmaik kaṭciyiṉ āṭcikālattil peṇkaḷukku 1921-l vāk kurimai vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatu, ciṟupāṉmaiyarukku nikarāḷiyam vaḻaṅkap paṭṭatu. 
1933ām āṇṭu ceṉṉai mānakarat talaivar (President) eṉṟa pataviyaic ceṉṉai mānakarattantai (Mayor of Madras Corporation) eṉṟum māṟṟiṉārkaḷ. eṉavē, talaivarāka irunta vayavar. irācā muttaiyā ceṭṭiyār ceṭṭināṭu aracar ceṉṉai mānakara mutal tantaiyāṉār. avar ci-3-1913 mutal 7-11-1933 varai mānakarat tantaiyākac ceyalpaṭṭār. ceṉṉai mānakara maṉṟam toṭaṅkiyati liruntu aṉṟu varai pirāmaṇarkaḷē ''mānakara maṉṟat talaivarkaḷāka iruntaṉar. ivar tāṉ mutal ''tantai'' eṉpatum * mutal pirāmaṇa rallātavar" eṉpatum kuṟippiṭattakkatu. varuppuvāripaṭinikarāḷiyampaṭi mutalil nakarattantaiyāṉavarum ivarēyāvār.vakuppuvāri paṭinikarāḷiya vitippaṭi mutaṉ mutalākac ceṉṉai mānakarat tantaiyāṉa mutal āti tirāviṭarcē. civacaṇmukampiḷḷai yāvār. 
1958ām āṇṭu ceṉṉai mānakarāṭciyil 50-liruntu 100 pirivukaḷ (wards) ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. atē āṇṭil nakaramutuvarkaḷ (Alderrhern) muṟaiyum oḻikkappaṭṭatu. 
1951-l ceṉṉai nakara nakariyam" (Madras City Municipality) eṉpatai "ceṉṉai nakariya mānakarāṭci " (Madras CityCorporation) eṉa māṟṟappaṭṭatu. 1973-l 100-liruntu 173 pirivukaḷāka (Wards) nakarāṭcip pirittāḷappaṭṭatu. 1945 ām āṇṭu potut tērtalukkuppiṉ 1957 varai pērāyakkaṭciyiṉ (kāṅkiracu āṭciyē toṭarntu iruntatu. ik kālakkaṭṭattiltāṉ uḷḷāṭcittuṟaikkeṉa taṉi amaicca cum" ēṟpaṭṭatu. "niyamaṉa muṟai" aṭiyōṭu oḻikkappaṭṭatu.) 
1953-l ceṉṉai makaḷaṭivattil mottamirunta 25 māvaṭṭak kaḻakaṅkaḷukkum, ō8 nakariyaṅkaḷukkum, ''nakaramaippuc caṭṭam" (Town Plarl ning Act) koṇṭuvarappaṭṭu, ataṉpaṭi orē mātiriyāṉa uḷḷāṭci amaippu ēṟpaṭṭatu. itaṉaṭippaṭaiyiltāṉ pañcāyattu irācciyam, kumukāya vaḷarccit tiṭṭaṅkaḷ, putiya pāñcāyattukkaḷai ēṟpaṭuttutal mutaliyaṉa tōṉṟi ""kumukāyanalamē nāṭṭiṉ nōkkam' eṉap pāṭupaṭat toṭaṅkiṉa.

ceṉṉai mānakarāṭciyil mānakarāṭcit talaivarkaḷ (Presidents) uṟuppiṉarkaḷāl tērnteṭukkappaṭṭaṉar. 1919 ām āṇṭu mutal 1932 ām āṇṭuvarai "'talaivarkaḷ" eṉṟa pataviyil pattu pēr irunta ṉar. 1933 ām āṇṭil talaivar eṉṟa patavi nakarat tantai (Mayor) eṉa māṟṟappaṭṭatu. mutal nakarat tantaiyāka vayavar. irācā muttaiyā ceṭṭiyār tērnteṭukkappaṭṭār.1933 mutal 2000 varai tērnteṭuk kappaṭṭa nakarat tantaiyarkaḷ vakuppuvārip paṭinikarāḷiyappaṭi tērn teṭukkappaṭṭaṉar. 200 mutal makkaḷē nēraṭiyāka mānakarat tantaiyait tērnteṭuttaṉar. avaruṭaiya patavik kālamum caṭṭamaṉṟa uṟuppiṉar kaḷaip pōl aintāṇṭukaḷāṉatu. avvāṟu mutaṉ mutalāka makkaḷāl tērnteṭukkap peṟṟavar ti.mu.ka.vaic cērnta mu.ka. sṭāliṉ (Mr.K. vakuppuvāri paṭinikarāḷiya vitippaṭi mēyar patavi vakikkappaṭṭa tāl intiyak kiṟittuvar, iculāmiyar, ātitirāviṭar mutaliyōrum mānakarat tantaiyākap patavi vakittaṉar. ātitirāviṭanakarattantaiyarkaḷil cē, civacaṇmukam piḷḷai (1937-38) eṉ. civarācu (1945-46), pi. paramēcuvaraṉ (1953-54), ci. kucēlar (1951-53), vi. pālacuntaram (1969.70) mutaliyōr kuṟippiṭattakkavarāvar, 
ciṟappāṉa ulaka mānakara maṉṟaṅkaḷaip pōl ciṟappuṟṟu viḷaṅkuvatu ceṉṉai mānakaramākum. mānakarap paṇikaḷil mikavum ciṟappāṉavaiyākak karutappaṭuvaṉa toṭakkakkalvi vaḷarcciyum, māṇavarukku matiya uṇavaḷikkum tiṭṭamum, kuṭicai māṟṟu vāriyamum, taṭaiyilāk kuṭinīr vaḻaṅkum muṟaiyum, ataṟkākac cattiyamūrtti pūṇṭi) nīrttēkkattaiyum, ceṅkuṉṟam ērinīrt tēkkattaiyum ēṟpaṭuttiyatum, ulakat tarattiṟku oppāṉa nakaraccālaikaḷ, cākkaṭai ēntukaḷ, mēmpālaṅkaḷ, pūṅkākkaḷ mutaliyaṉavumākum, kacappāṉa ceyti, mānakarap paṇikaḷ araciyal kaṭcikaḷiṉ kāḻppu uṇarvukaḷāl cila camayam cemmaik keṭṭu niṟpatākum. 
uḷḷāṭci vaḷarcci kālantōṟum kālattiṟkēṟpa vaḷarntu koṇṭē vantu makkaḷ cēvaiyē makēcaṉ cēvai" eṉṟa tattuvattil ceyalpaṭukiṟatu. itaṉāltāṉ kipi. 1934-l vaṭṭāṭcik kaḻakaṅkaḷ (Taluk Boards) mūṭappaṭṭu māvaṭṭak kaḻakaṅkaḷukku (District Ecards) mukāmaiyaḷikkappaṭṭatu. piṉṉar māvaṭṭak kaḻakaṅkaḷ 1953-l mūṭap paṭṭu, kālattiṟkēṟpa vaṭṭāra vaḷarcci aluvalakaṅkaḷ ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. intiyāviṉ uyirnāṭi kirāmaṅkaḷē eṉṟa kāntiyāriṉ viruppappaṭi pañcāyattukkaḷum, pañcāyattu rācciyamum ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. aintāṇṭut tiṭṭak kālaṅkaḷil viraintu vaḷarccip peṟṟaṉa. 1959.79 kālakkaṭṭat til ivaṟṟiṟku āṇṭuttiṭṭaṅkaḷum, valuvāṉa nitiyum ēṟpaṭṭaṉa.

tāynila varalāṟu 
''rippaṉ eṅkaḷ appaṉ"" eṉṟu intiyar aṉaivarālum pōṟṟip pukaḻappaṭum rippaṉ piraputāṉ navīṉa uḷḷāṭci muṟaiyai intiyāvil aṟimukappaṭuttiṉār. avar pāmarap potumakkaḷum āṭcimuṟaiyil paṅku peṟṟup payiṟci peṟuvatē inta uḷḷāṭciyiṉ nōkkameṉṟār. avaruṭaiya kaṉavu iṉṟu muḻumaiyāka niṟaivēṟi viṭṭatu. vākkāḷarkaḷukkuc cotturimai irukka vēṇṭumeṉṟa viti maṟaintu, vayatu vantōr ellōrukkum vākkurimai ēṟpaṭṭatu. 1909 ām āṇṭu ēṟpaṭṭa araciyal caṭṭappaṭi kiṟittuvar. iculāmiyar, cīkkiyar mutaliya ciṟupāṉmaiyiṉarukkum, 1919 ām āṇṭilēṟpaṭṭa intiya araciyal caṭṭappaṭi tāḻttappaṭṭavarukkum uḷḷāṭci maṉṟaṅ kaḷilum, caṭṭa nāṭāḷum maṉṟaṅkaḷilum paṭinikarāḷiyam koṭukkap paṭṭatu. 
1950 ām āṇṭiliruntu naṭaimuṟaikku vanta intiya araciyal caṭṭattilēyē uḷḷāṭcimuṟaiyum kuṟippākap pañcāyattu irācciyam mum oru pakutiyākac cērkkappaṭṭaṉa. eṉavē iṉṟu cāti camaya vēṟupā ṭiṉṟi intiyar evarum uḷḷāṭci maṉṟaṅkaḷil paṅkēṟkalām. ittakaiyap payiṟciyāl caṭṭamaṉṟa, nāṭāḷum maṉṟaṅkaḷilum amaiccar, kuṭiyaracuttalaivar pōṉṟa uyarpatavikaḷilum evarum varalām. eṉavētāṉ, rippaṉ cuṇṭa kaṉavu iṉṟu naṉavākiviṭṭatu eṉkiṟōm. 
' iṉṉamum kuṟippiṭṭa iṉattiṟkuppaṭinikarāḷikaḷai niyamaṉam" ceyyum muṟaiyum uḷḷatu.tāḻttappaṭṭavaraippōlavē makaḷirukkum iṭa otukkīṭu ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷatu. pala pañcāyattukkaḷil makaḷirum talaivarkaḷāka uḷḷaṉar. atilum āti tirāviṭa makaḷirukkeṉṟu taṉi iṭaotukkīṭu uṇṭu. āyiṉum, pala āyiram āṇṭukaḷākac cātippirivukaḷ ḷālum, tīṇṭāmaiyālum vāṭiya intiyar putitāka araciyal kaṭcikaḷ' eṉṟa pēyāl āṭṭippaṭaikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. caṭṭamaṉṟa nāṭāḷumaṉṟat tērtalkaḷil maṭṭum araciyal kaṭcikaḷiṉ peyarāl vēṭpāḷar pōṭṭiyiṭuvatu nallatu. uḷḷāṭci maṉṟaṅkaḷil araciyalai aṭiyōṭu oḻittāl uḷḷāṭci oḷimayamāṉatākat tikaḻum, 
viṭutalaikkuppiṉ tamiḻakam 
(1947 mutal iṉṟu varai) 
muṉṉurai 
1947 ām āṇṭu ākacṭu 15 ām nāḷ āṅkilēyariṉ aṭimaip piṭiyiliruntu pāratanāṭu viṭutalaiyaṭaintatu. āṉāl, viṭutalaikkup piṉ potuvāka intiyāvilum, kuṟippākat tamiḻnāṭṭilum amaiti yāṉa cūḻnilai illai. aṉṟu tamiḻakam taṉittiyaṅkavillai. ceṉṉai maṇṭilattiṉ oru pakutiyākavē iruntatu. āntiramum, kēraḷattiṉ oru pakutiyum, kaṉṉaṭattiṉ oru pakutiyum im maṇṭilattil iṇaintu iruntaṉa. eṉavē, tamiḻ, teluṅku, malaiyāḷam, oriyā, kaṉṉaṭam ākiya moḻi pēcum makkaḷum, ciṟupāṉmai moḻiyiṉarum iṅkiruntaṉar. nāṭu viṭutalai peṟṟavuṭaṉ im makkaḷ moḻivaḻi mānilaṅkaḷ piriya ' vēṇṭumeṉa virumpiṉar.pērāntiram vicāla āntiram vēṇṭumeṉak kōri āntirar muṭukkamāṉap pōrāṭṭam toṭuttaṉar. itaṉai muṉ vaittup poṭṭi ciṟīrāmulu' eṉṟa āntira tēciyavāti, taṉ uyiraiyum viṭṭār. kalakam kaṭṭukkaṭaṅkāmal pōkavē āntirattait tamiḻakatti viruntu taṉiyē pirittu, 1.10.1953-l āntira mānilam uruvākkiṉar. ' 
, āntirattaiyaṭuttut tamiḻakattilum āntirattōṭum, kēraḷat tōṭum iṇaintu irunta ceṉṉai maṇṭilat tamiḻppakutikaḷaip pirittut tamiḻakattōṭu iṇaittut taṉittamiḻnāṭu vēṇṭumeṉa vaṭa tamiḻ nāṭṭil tamiḻaracukkaḻakam' teṉ tamiḻnāṭṭil "tāyt tamiḻaka iyakkam' mutaliyaṉavum pōrāṭiṉa. pērarntiram pirikkappaṭumpōtu cittūr māvaṭṭam muḻuvatum ataṉuṭaṉ iṇaikkappaṭṭuviṭṭatu, atiluḷḷa tiruttaṇi, tirukkāḷatti, pallavaṉēṟi, kaṅkuntikkuppam, cittūr ākiya māvaṭṭaṅkaḷilum, tamiḻarkaḷ vacippatāl, avaṟṟaiyum tamiḻakattōṭu cērkkavēṇṭumeṉat tamiḻaracukkaḻakam pōrāṭiyatu. kaṭaiciyil cittūr tavirtta maṟṟavaṟṟil cila pakutikaḷ tamiḻakattuṭaṉ iṇaikkappaṭṭaṉa. itaṉāl vaṭakkellaiyil oruvāṟu amaiti ēṟpaṭṭatu. 
teṟkellaiyil akattīcuvaram, tōvāḷai, kalkuḷam, viḷavaṅ kōṭu mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷilum tiruvitāṅkūr maṉṉarāṭciyiṉ kīḻum

1pērāyak kaṭci āṭciyiṉ cātaṉaikaḷ (1947-1967) 
1935 ām āṇṭu intiya araciyal caṭṭappaṭi 1937-l naṭanta tērtalil pērāyak kaṭci (kāṅkiras veṟṟippeṟṟu, 1937 mutal 1939 varai 18 tiṅkaḷ ci. irācakōpālāccāriyār talaimaiyil amaic caravai amaittu āṇṭatu. iraṇṭām ulakappōril intiyat talaivarkaḷaik kalantōcikkāmal piriṭṭāṉiyar intiyāvaiyum pōrilīṭupaṭuttiyatāl etirpput terivittu, pērāyak kaṭcit tāṉē amaiccaravaiyaik kalaittuk koṇṭatu. 
itaṟkuppiṉ 1939 mutal 1945varaip pōrkkāla āṭci allatu iṭaikkāla āṭci naṭantatu. tēciya viṭutalaip pōriṉ tākkattāl 1946-l tērtal naṭantatu. atil pērāyak kaṭciyē veṟṟippeṟṟu ceṉṉai maṇṭilattil āṭci amaittatu. teluṅkarāṉ pirakācam talaimaiyil 30 4.14-l amaiccaravai amaintatu. moḻic cikkalāl pērāyak kaṭciyilum uṭkaṭcippūccilēṟpaṭṭatu. itaṉaṭippaṭaiyil pirakācam14.3.1947-l patavi tuṟantār. avaraiyaṭuttut tamiḻarāṉa ō.pi. irāmacāmi reṭṭiyār mutalamaiccarāṉār. avar 2331947liruntu 1949 varai mutalamaiccar patavi vakittār. ivar mutalamaiccarāka irunta poḻututāṉ intiyāvukku viṭutalai {75-8-1947) kiṭaittatu. cātaṉaikaḷ 
ivaruṭaiya āṭcikkālattil potuvuṭaimaikkārarkaḷ pala puraṭcikaḷaic ceytaṉar. avaṟṟai aṭakkuvataṟkākavē potu amaitip pēṇum caṭṭam koṇṭu varappaṭṭatu; uṇavuppaṟṟāk kuṟaiyaic camāḷikka niyāya vilaik kaṭaikaḷ ēṟpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa; kāykaṟi uṟpatti atikappaṭuttappaṭṭatu; pācaṉa ēntukaḷ atikarikkap paṭṭaṉa; ātitirāviṭar nalattai mēmpaṭuttat toḻilāḷar nalat" tuṟaiyai iraṇṭākap pirittu aricaṉa nalattuṟai'yai ēṟpaṭuttiṉār; 1947l kōyil nuḻaivuccaṭṭam koṇṭuvantār; putiya kalvik kōṭ pāṭṭāl maṉarppurakkuḻantaikaḷukkum kalvip payaṉ kiṭaikkappeṟṟatu. , 
pi. ecu. kumāracāmirācā (1949-1952) cātaṉaikaḷ 
nayaṉmaikkaṭciyiṉ āṭcik kālattil koṇṭuvarappaṭṭa ''vakuppuvārip paṭinikarāḷiya āṇai" 1951-l ucca nayaṉmai maṉṟattāl

taḷḷupaṭiyāṉatu. kumāracāmirācāviṉ perum muyaṟciyāl mīṇṭum atu uyirpeṟṟatu mutal aintāṇṭut tiṭṭam (1951-1956 niṟaivēṟiyatu. itil atikat tokai kalvikkuc celavaḻikkappaṭṭatu: "vayatuvantōrukku vākkurimai" (Adult Suffarage) ēṟpaṭṭatu. 
ci. irācakōpālāccāriyār (irācāci - 1952-1954) 
1952-l intiyāviṉ mutal potuttērtal naṭantatu. pērāyak kaṭciyē veṟṟip peṟṟatu. cakkaravartti irācakōpālāccāriyār 
mutalamaiccarāṉār. cātaṉaikaḷ 
* "kulakkalvit tiṭṭam' * koṇṭuvarappaṭṭatu. itaṉai etirttu nāṭu muḻuvatum naṭanta kiḷarccikaḷāl atu kaiviṭappaṭṭatu, inta etirppiṉ etiroliyāka irācāci patavit tuṟantār. kulakkalvit tiṭṭattāl palanūṟu paḷḷikaḷ mūṭappaṭṭaṉa. pala āciriyarkaḷ vēlai iḻantaṉar. 
ku. kāmarācar (1954-1963) - irācāci patavi tuṟantatum, tamiḻnāṭupērāyak kaṭcit talaivarāṉa ku. kāmarācar mutalvarāṉār. mutaṉmuṟaiyāka 1958 mutal 1957 varai mutalamaiccar patavi vakittār. 1957-l naṭanta nāṭṭiṉ iraṇṭāvatu potut tērtalil pērāyakkaṭciyē mīṇṭum veṟṟippeṟṟatāl 1957 mutal 16 varai iraṇṭām muṟaiyākak kāmarācar mutalamaiccarāṉār, 1952-l naṭanta mūṉṟāvatu potut tērtalilum pērāyak kaṭciyē veṟṟip peṟṟatāl mūṉṟāvatu muṟaiyākak kāmarācarē mutalamaicca rāṉār. 1952 mutal 1963 varai mutalamaiccarāyiruntu, mūtta kaṭcit toṇṭarkaḷ patavi vilakik kaṭcippaṇikaḷaic ceyya vēṇṭumeṉat tāḷē oru tiṭṭattai vakuttu mutalamaiccar pataviyait tuṟantuveḷi yēṟiya avarai aṉaittintiyap pērāyak kaṭciyiṉ talaivarākkiṉar. avaruṭaiya tiṭṭattai avaruṭaiya peyarālēyē "kāmarācar tiṭṭam" eṉṟaḻaittaṉar, cātaṉaikaḷ 
kalvippuraṭci : ci. irācakōpālāccāriyār (irācāci) avarkaḷāl mūṭappaṭṭap paḷḷikkūṭaṅkaḷaik kāmarācar tiṟantār; * "ellōrukkum kalvi'' eṉṟa taṉ neñcārnta kōṭpāṭṭaic ceyalpaṭutta ārtōṟum paḷḷikaḷait tōṟṟuvittār. 1955-56-l eṭṭām vakuppuvarai ellōrukkum ilavacak kalviyaḷittār. 1950-500l aintu kallukku oru uyarnilaip paḷḷit tiṭṭattiṉ kīḻ uyarnilaip paḷḷikaḷaiyum mūṉṟu kal cuṟṟuvārattiṟkuḷ oru toṭakkap paḷḷiyum toṭaṅkiṉār. .

irācāciyiṉ āṭcikkālattil uyar nilaippaḷḷiyil paṭittavar eṇṇikkai 31900 māṇavarkaḷākum. kāmarācar āṭcikkālattil itu 7,15,000 āka uyarntatu. kāmarācar kalvikkuc celaviṭum tokaiyē nāṭṭiṉ celavut tokaiyil mutaṉmaiyāṉatu. itaikkaṇṭa potu makkaḷ tāṅkaḷē muṉvantu ēḻukōṭi urupā naṉkoṭaiyāka aḷittaṉar. culḷivīṭutōṟum ceṉṟaṭaintatu. 
19-5-l 40 narkaḷil kaṭṭāyak kalviyaic ceyalpaṭuttiṉār. mutal vakuppu mutalē āṅkilam paṭikkac ceytār. itaṉāl paṭṭap paṭippuppaṭitta āciriyarkaḷ atikamāka āciriyar paṇiyil amarttap paṭṭaṉar. āciriyar vāḻkkait taram uyarttappaṭṭatu. toḻilkalvi, maruttuvakkalvi mutaliyaṉa mēmpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa.kōvaiyilum, maturai yilum palkalaikkaḻakaṅkaḷ toṭaṅkappaṭṭaṉa. āciriyap payiṟcik kalluārikaḷ ēḻiliruntu patiṉēḻāka uyarntaṉa. āciriyappayiṟcip paḷḷikaḷ 107viruntu 138 āka uyarntaṉa. poṟiyiyal kallūrikaḷil māṇavar eṇṇikkai atikarikkappaṭṭatu. paḷḷi māṇavarkaḷukku ilavaca matiya uṇavut tiṭṭam mukāmaip peṟṟatu. ataṟkāka atikap paṇam celaviṭappaṭṭatu, tamiḻ āṭci moḻiyātal - ku. kāmarācar iraṇṭām muṟaiyākat tamiḻakattiṉ mutalvarāṉa vuṭaṉ tamiḻ moḻiyē tamiḻakattiṉ āṭcimoḻiyāṉatu. ataṟkāṉa āṭcimoḻic caṭṭamuṉvaraivai kalvi amaiccar ci. cuppiramaṇiyam caṭṭamaṉṟattiṉ muṉ vaittup pēcumpōtu kaṭanta aimpatu, aṟupatu āṇṭukaḷāka aṟiñar perumakkaḷ ayarātu uḻaittatiṉ palaṉākavē, iṉṟu tamiḻ tamiḻaka niruvākat tuṟaiyai āḷappōkiṟatu. avarkaḷiṉ uḻaippāl iṉṟup pūttuk kuluṅkum tamiḻ nāḷaik kāyākik kaṉiyākit tamiḻarkaḷukku iṉcuvai amutāyp payaṉpaṭappōkiṟatu. itaṟkāka, āṭcimuṟaiyil eppaṭi tamiḻaic ceyalpaṭuttuvatu eṉpatai āynturaikka " āṭci moḻi āyvukkuḻu" oṉṟai ēṟpaṭuttiṉōm. akkuḻu, aracuttuṟaikaḷil aluvalakaṅkaḷil tamiḻmoḻiyaic ceyal paṭuttum vaḻivakaikaḷai āyntu aracukku aḷittatu" eṉṟār. 
mutalil ik kuḻuviṉar tayāritta āṭciyāḷarkaḷ (tuṟaiyil . paṇiyāṟṟuvōr) kaiyāḷum āṅkilac coṟkaḷukku iṇaiyāṉa tamiḻccoṟkaḷai uruvākki * āṭciccol akarāti" (Glossary of Administrative Terrms) (English-Tamil) eṉṟa oru nūlākat tamiḻaka aracu 1966-l veḷiyiṭṭatu. itaṉai mēlum cemmaippaṭutti, aṭuttu vanta timuka aracum veḷiyiṭṭatu. 
āṭcimoḻit tiṭṭac ceyalpāṭu 
tamiḻai āṭci moḻiyāka ākkum caṭṭattaip pērāyak kaṭci āṭci 1957 ām āṇṭu, caṉavarit tiṅkaḷ 21 ām nāḷ koṇṭuvantatu.

tāynilavaralāṟu 
ic caṭṭattaic ceyalpaṭutta aracu oru kuḻuvai amarttic ceyalmuṟai naṭavaṭikkaikaḷai aḷikkac ceytatu. ik kuḻuviṉ muyaṟciyāl veḷi yiṭappaṭṭatē mēlē kūṟappaṭṭa ''āṭcic col akarāti"yākum. 
aṭuttu, āṭci aluvalakaṅkaḷiluḷḷa eḻuttarkaḷ, pirivut talaivarkaḷukku ituvaraiyil āṅkilattil eḻuti vanta muṟaiyai māṟṟi 
eppaṭit tamiḻil eḻutuvatu yeṉpataṟkup payiṟci aḷikkappaṭṭatu. ituvarai āṅkilat taṭṭaccukkaḷē payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. avaṟṟiṟku īṭākat tamiḻt taṭṭaccukkaḷaip payaṉpaṭuttat toṭaṅkiṉar. 
tamiḻ teriyāta paṇiyāḷarkaḷukkut tamiḻil eḻutavum, paṭikkavum payiṟci aḷikkappaṭṭatu. ittakaiya māṟṟattiṉpōtu paṇiyāḷar cuḷukku aiyam ēṟpaṭāmalirukkac cariyāṉa tamiḻkkōppukaḷait tayārikka aluvalakaṅkaḷilēyē moḻipeyarppup pirivu" (Tarislation Sction) ēṟpaṭṭatu. 
1955ām āṇṭilēyē āṅkilac curukkeḻuttu muṟaiyaip pōlavē tamiḻc curukkeḻuttu muṟai veḷiyiṭappaṭṭatu. itai naṭai muṟaippaṭutta aracē itaṟkāka oru ciṟanta payiṟci nilaiyattai uruvākkiyatu. itaṉāl tamiḻc curukkeḻuttāḷarum, taṭṭaccāḷarum perumaḷavil aluvalakap paṇikaḷil amarttappaṭṭaṉar. 
mānilat tērvāṇaiyattāl tērvu ceyyappeṟum paṇiyāḷar kaḷukkut tamiḻil kōppukaḷait tayārittal, tamiḻil taṭṭaccu ceytal mutaliyavaṟṟil payiṟci aḷikkappaṭṭaṉa. naṭuvaṇ āṭciyāḷarkaḷākat tērnteṭukkap peṟum intiya āṭcippaṇiyāḷarkaḷ (IAS) intiyak kāval tuṟaip paṇiyāḷarkaḷ (I.P.S), mutaliyōrukkum, vēṟṟumoḻip paṇiyāḷarkaḷukkum āṭcit tuṟaiyil tamiḻil paṭikkavum, eḻutavum ciṟappāṉa payiṟci aḷikkappaṭṭatu. 
tamiḻaka aracuppaṇiyil cēruvataṟkup pōtiya tamiḻaṟivu irukka vēṇṭumeṉṟum aracu āṇai piṟappittatu. 1957-l tamiḻaka aracup paṇiyil putitākac cēruvataṟkum, patavi uyarvu peṟuvataṟkum, māṟṟup paṇiyil cēruvataṟkum mānila aḷavu tamiḻ aṟivut tēvaiyeṉum 
āṇai piṟantatu. ataṟkāṉat tuṟaittērvum naṭattappaṭṭatu. 
1961 ām āṇṭu cōtaṉai aṭippaṭaiyil intuccamaya aṟa nilaiya āṭcit tuṟaiyiṉ tuṇai āṇaiyar aluvalakaṅkaḷ, mīṉ vaṉattuṟai utavi iyakkunar aluvalakam, pativuttuṟaiyil māvaṭṭap pativāḷar aluvalakam uḷḷiṭṭa uyarnilai aluvalakaṅkaḷilum, nitittaṇikkait tuṟaiyilum, tamiḻil aluvalkaḷ naṭaipeṟat toṭaṅkiṉa. 
1961 ām āṇṭu mutalē māvaṭṭa nilaiyil amaintuḷḷa palvēṟu tuṟaikaḷilum, cārnilai aluvalakaṅkaḷilum tamiḻil aluvalakap paṇikaḷ toṭaṅkiviṭṭaṉa. 1953-l 38 tuṟaikaḷil tamiḻ āṭcimoḻiyāṉatu.

1. tamiḻ āṭcimoḻit tiṭṭattai aracu aluvalakaṅkaḷil ceyalpaṭutta āvaṉa ceyyavum, aracāṇaikaḷ aṟikkaikaḷ, 
aṟivuṟuttalkaḷ veḷiyiṭavum ikkuḻu āvaṉa ceytatu. 2 palvēṟu cuṟṟaṟikkaikaḷaip piṟappittut tamiḻ āṭcimoḻit " tiṭṭattai vaḷarttatu. 
3. aluvalakaṅkaḷil payaṉpaṭuttappaṭum caṭṭaṅkaḷ, vitikaḷ, naṭaimuṟai nūlkaḷ, paṭivaṅkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟait tamiḻil moḻi peyarttatu. 4. tamiḻt tiṭṭam ceyalpaṭuttappaṭum aluvalakaṅkaḷiṉ ceyal 
pāṭṭu nilaikaḷai āyvu ceytu aṟivurai nalkiyatu. 5. āṭcimoḻikkuḻuviṉ talaivarāyiruntavaraiyē mānila muḻumaikkum taṉi aluvalarāka amarttiṉar. piṉṉar ip patavi iyakkunar pataviyāka (Director of Tamil Levelopment) māṟiyatu. ivar aṉaittintiyap paṇiyāḷarāvār. ivarukkuk kīḻppala aluvalar amarttappaṭṭaṉar. avarkaḷ moḻipeyarpput toṭarpāṉa paṇikaḷ, āyvup paṇikaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟai mēṟkoṇṭaṉar. 
caṅka kālattil āṭci moḻiyāka irunta tamiḻ kālappōkkil nilaimai iḻantu pāli, camaṟkirutam, pāracīkam, arāpi, urutu, pōrttu kīciya, āṅkila moḻikaḷāl tākkuṇṭatu. āṅkilēyar kālattil oru paranta nilap parappil āṭci amaippuc cīrpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. āṉāl, āṭci moḻi āṅkilamākavē iruntatu. mēlai nāṭṭuk kalvimuṟai yāl aṉaivarum kalvik kaṟkum vāyppu ēṟpaṭṭatu. tamiḻ tāḻntatu. 
1835-l mekkālēpirapu intiyarukku āṅkilak kalviyaik kaṟpikkac ceytār. āṅkilak kalviyāl mēnāṭṭu āṭcimuṟai, aṟiviyal, paṇpāṭukaḷ intiyarāl aṟiyappaṭṭaṉa. itaṉāl tāy moḻippaṟṟu mēliṭṭatu, tamiḻaṟiñarkaḷ tamiḻai āṭcimoḻiyākka vēṇṭumeṉavīriṭṭeḻuntaṉar, 
camaṟkirutam tamiḻait taṉ mayamākkik koṇṭatai māṟṟit taṉittamiḻaiuyirppikkac ceytaṉar.paritimāṟ kalaiñar, tiruvika vēta nāyakam piḷḷai , maṟaimalai aṭikaḷār, pārati, pāratitācaṉ cōm cuntara pāratiyār, kācupiḷḷai , vipulāṉantar mutaliya eṇṇiṟanta tamiḻaṟiñarkaḷ tamiḻai ariyaṇaiyēṟṟap pāṭupaṭṭaṉar. 
pala kaḻakaṅkaḷum, pala iyakkaṅkaḷum tamiḻai āṭcimoḻiyākka muṭukkamāyk kiḷampiṉa.kālamum kaṉintatu. 1920 ām āṇṭilēṟpaṭṭa iraṭṭai āṭcimuṟaiyil makkaḷiṉ paṭinikarāḷikaḷ nāṭṭai āḷat toṭaṅkiṉar. caṭṭamaṉṟaṅkaḷil tamiḻk kural olittatu. 
tamiḻil caṭṭamaṉṟaṅkaḷil pēcattoṭaṅkiṉar. iṉṟiyamaiyāta cila caṭṭaṅkaḷ āṅkilattiliruntu tamiḻil peyarkkappaṭṭaṉa. "moḻi

vaḻi mānila āṭci" eṉṟa karuttuc caṭṭamaṉṟattil1927lēyē olikkat toṭaṅkiviṭṭatu. 
1937-liruntu 1939 varai ci. irācakōpālāccāriyār pērāyak kaṭciyiṉ mutalamaiccarāka tamiḻakattil pataviyil iruntār. tamiḻ, teluṅku, kaṉṉaṭam, malaiyāḷam, ōriyā ākiyavai ceṉṉai maṇṭilat tiliruntālum āṭci moḻitāymoḻitāṉ" eṉpatu appoḻutu uṟutiyāyiṟṟu. 
intiya viṭutalaikkuppiṉ 1956-l āntiram moḻiyiṉaṭip paṭaiyil pirintatu. 1956-l "moḻivaḻi mānilaṅkaḷ piṟantaṉa. 
ci. irācakōpālāccāriyār mutalvarāyirunta pōtu tirucci māvaṭṭat til 1952l muṉṉōṭittiṭṭamākat tamiḻ āṭcimoḻiyāka aṟivikkappaṭṭatu. āṉāl taḷḷip pōṭappaṭṭatu. 1956-l ku. kāmarācar mutalvarāyiruntapōtu, kalvi amaiccar ci. cuppiramaṇiyam tamiḻ āṭcimoḻic caṭṭattaik koṇṭu vantu ataic ceyal paṭuttiṉār. 1957 varaip pērāyak kaṭci āṇṭa kālattilēyē 
mēṟkaṇṭavāṟu āṭci moḻiyāki, valuppeṟṟu viṭṭatu tamiḻ. potu cukātāra mēmpāṭu 
kāmarācar kālattil iraṇṭām, mūṉṟām aintāṇṭut tiṭṭaṅkaḷ ceyalpaṭuttap peṟṟaṉa. 1951-l kumukāya nalakkaḻakam, makaḷir nalakkaḻakam, ākiya iraṇṭum iṇaikkappaṭṭaṉa. piṉṉar iḵtu ''kumukāya nalattuṟai"(Social Welfare Department) eṉum orē tuṟaiyākac ceyalpaṭṭatu. ivar kālattil 1959-l * intu aṟanilaiyac caṭṭam" tirutti amaikkappaṭṭatu. ic caṭṭattāl arccakar patavi paramparai 
urimaiyākkappaṭṭatu." 
(a) vēḷāṇmai poruḷātāra mēmpāṭu: 
tamiḻaka makkaḷ tokaiyil 50 viḻukkāṭu vēḷāṇmaiyilum, mītamuḷḷa 40 viḻukkāṭu piṟa toḻilkaḷilum īṭupaṭṭuḷḷaṉar. mēlum tamiḻaka makkaḷtokaikku ēṟpa vilainilaṅkaḷ illai. vēḷāṇmait toḻilil īṭupaṭṭuḷḷavarkaḷil 43 viḻukkāṭṭiṉar vēḷāṇ kūlikaḷā yuḷḷaṉar. kāraṇam pōtiya nilavuṭaimaikkārarkaḷ illai . 1948-l "perunila uṭamaikkārar oḻippuc caṭṭam '' (jamīṉ oḻippuc caṭṭam koṇṭuvarappaṭṭatālum, 191l nila uccavarampuc caṭṭam koṇṭu varappaṭṭatālum tamiḻakattil ōraḷavuuḻavarkaḷ eṇṇikkai atikarit tatu. 1948, 1959 ām āṇṭukaḷil koṇṭu varappaṭṭa "paṇṇaiyāḷ kūli nirṇayac caṭṭaṅkaḷ'', 1952, 195ām āṇṭukaḷil koṇṭuvarap paṭṭa "uḻavar nalac caṭṭaṅkaḷ ākiyavai uḻavarkaḷiṉ naṉmaikkum, pātukāppukkum mēmpāṭu aḷittaṉa.

tāynila varalāṟu 
mutal, iraṇṭu, mūṉṟu aintāṇṭut tiṭṭak kālaṅkaḷil uṇavu uṟpattikku mutaliṭam aḷittatāl attiṭṭaṅkaḷiṉ muṭivil muṟaiyē 3094, 5,000 kalleṭaiṭaṉ tavaca uṟpatti ēṟpaṭṭatu. ikkāla vēḷāṇmai muṟaiyil navīṉa muṟaikaḷ kaiyāḷappaṭṭaṉa. vētiyiyal muṟaiyil tayārāṉa uraṅkaḷ, pūccikkolli maruntukaḷ, poṟukku vitaikaḷ mutaliyaṉa payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. tīvira vēḷāṇmait tiṭṭam 
ittiṭṭam pōrṭu amaippiṉ oru vaḻikāṭṭiṭattiṭṭamākum. cōtaṉai muṟaiyil tañcai māvaṭṭattil 1950-l it tiṭṭam toṭaṅkappaṭṭatu. ēṉeṉṟāl im māvaṭṭattiltāṉ 14 ilakkam kuṟukkattil ēkkaril nel payiriṭappaṭukiṉṟatu. tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ nel uṟpattiyil nāṉkil oru paṅku nel immāvaṭṭattil uṟpattiyākiṟatu. 
taṉippaṭṭa kuṭiyāṉavaṉ mutaṟkoṇṭu ūr, māvaṭṭam, mānilam, naṭuvaṇ āṭcikaḷ varai kūṭṭuṟavu muṟaiyil uṇavu uṟpattip perukkum muṟaikaḷaik kaiyāṇṭu uṇavu uṟpattiyaip perukkuvatē intat tīvira vēḷāṇmait tiṭṭattiṉ mukāmai nōkkamākum. kuṭiyāṉavaṉiṉ āṇṭu varumāṉattai atikarikkac ceytu ataṉvaḻi avaṉ vāḻum palariṉ varumāṉattaiyum atikappaṭuttiṉāl vēḷāṇ camait tuṟaiyiṉ poruḷātāram mēmpaṭuvatōṭu ovvoru ūrum taṉṉiṟaivup peṟumeṉpatum, itaṉāl vēḷāṇmaiyil makkaḷukku naṇakkam ēṟpaṭum eṉpatum ittiṭṭattiṉ kuṟikkōḷākum. ittiṭṭat tiṉāl uṟpatti 20 viḻukkāṭu varai atikarikkumeṉṟu kaṇakkiṭap paṭṭatu. vivacāyak kaṭaṉutavi 
vēḷāṇmaiyai kākkuvippataṟkāka vēḷāḷarukkuk kaṭaṉutavit tiṭṭamum aṟimukappaṭuttappaṭṭatu. 1956-ām āṇṭukaḷil, atāvatu iraṇṭāvatu aintu āṇṭut tiṭṭak kālattil 20 kōṭi rūpāy tamiḻaka vivacāyikaḷukkuk kaṭaṉaḷikkappaṭṭatu. 1962 mutal ituvē eṇpatu kōṭiyāka uyarttappaṭṭatu. itaṉai araciṭamiruntu peṟum 
vaḻivakaikaḷ eḷitākkappaṭṭaṉa. nīrppācaṉa ēntukaḷ 
vēḷāṇmaikku uyirnāṭiyāṉa nīrppācaṉam tamiḻakattil kāmarācarkālattil atikappaṭuttappaṭṭatu. nīrppācaṉaṅkaḷ 1. kīḻpavāṉit tiṭṭam 
it tiṭṭattiṟkāka urupā pattu kōṭi celaviṭappaṭṭatu. itaṉāl kōvai māvaṭṭattil iraṇṭu ilakkattu ēḻu āyiram kuṟukkam nilapparappu nīrppācaṉam peṟṟatu.

it tiṭṭattiṟkāka aracu 195 ilakkam urupā celaviṭṭatu, itaṉāl cēlam, kōvai māvaṭṭaṅkaḷiluḷḷa 45 āyiram kuṟukkam nilapparappu payaṉaṭaintatu. 
iḵtu 1959-l cuṭṭi muṭikkappaṭṭatu. inta nīrttēkkattāl maturai māvaṭṭattil 2000 kuṟukkam nilapparappup pācaṉam peṟṟup payaṉaṭaikiṟatu. itaṟkāka aracu 330 kōṭi urupā celaviṭṭatu. 4. amarāvati nīrttēkkam

ciṟiya nīrppācaṉaṅkaḷ 
mēlē kuṟippiṭṭa periya nīrppācaṉat tiṭṭaṅkaḷait tavira pal ciṟiya nīrppācaṉat tiṭṭaṅkaḷum kāmarācar āṭcik kālattil niṟai vēṟiṉa. 
tamiḻakattil tōrāyamāka 27,000 ēri, kuḷaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟaic cīrpaṭuttak kāmarācar kālattil urupā eṭṭu kōṭi celavāṉatu. kuṟippāka mūṉṟāvatu aintāṇṭut tiṭṭakkālattiṟkumuṉ (1951-1956) paḻaiya ērikaḷum, kuḷaṅkaḷum cīrpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. putiya kiṇaṟukaḷ veṭṭappaṭṭaṉa, 
mēlum, vēḷāṇmai nīrppācaṉattiṟku vēṇṭiya nīr iraikkum iyantiraṅkaḷ tārāḷamākak koṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. itaṉāl tamiḻakattil uḷḷa payiriṭappaṭum150 kuṟukkam ilakkam nilattil mūṉṟilorupaṅku nilaiyākap payiriṭum vakaiyil nīrppācaṉa ēntukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. āṟṟu nīrppācaṉam tōrāyamāka 150 ilakkam kuṟukkam nilapparappil naṭakkiṟatu. 
(ā) toḻil mēmpāṭu poruḷātāra vaḷarccikku vēḷāṇmaikku aṭuttu mikavum mukāmai vāyntatu toḻil vaḷarcciyākum. toḻilkaḷ yāvum navīṉa toḻiṟcālaikaḷiltāṉ vaḷarcci aṭaikiṉṟaṉa. eṉavē ataṉaik kaṉarakat toḻiṟcālaikaḷ vaḷarcci yeṉṟum, ciṟurakat toḻiṟ cālaikaḷ vaḷarcciyeṉṟum, pirittaṟiyalām. ivai iraṇṭumē aracē ēṟṟu naṭattum toḻiṟcālaikaḷeṉṟum, taṉiyār ēṟṟu naṭattum toḻiṟcālaikaḷeṉṟum pirittaṟiyalām. eṉavē, tamiḻakattil toḻil vaḷarcci intiyāvilē mūṉṟāvatu iṭattaip piṭittuḷḷatu. 
1955-l irāmanātapuram ālaṅkuḷattil tamiḻakat toḻil vaḷarcci niṟuvaṉam toṭaṅkat tiṭṭamiṭappaṭṭatu. 1970-l iṅkuc cimeṇṭu toḻiṟcālai toṭaṅkappaṭṭatu. inta niṟuvaṉam 47 toḻil tiṭṭaṅkaḷaik koṇṭuḷḷatu. mutal aintāṇṭut tiṭṭak - kālattil urupā 12 ilakkamum, iraṇṭām aintāṇṭut tiṭṭak kālattil urupā 396 ilakkamum ciṟutoḻil vaḷarccikku otukkap paṭṭaṉa. iraṇṭām intāṇṭut tiṭṭak kālattil urupā 154 ilakkattil toḻil pēṭṭaikaḷai uruvākka otukkappaṭṭaṉa. attakaiya toḻiṟpēṭṭaikaḷ kiṇṭi, virutunakar, nellai, mārttāṇṭam, maturai, tirucci, kāṭṭuppāṭi, cēlam, ampattūr mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil uruvākkappaṭṭaṉa. kaittaṟikaḷ 
tamiḻakattil mottam 427 ilakkam kaittaṟikaḷ uḷḷaṉa. 1956-l * 'kaittaṟi necavāḷar kūṭṭuṟavu caṅkam"" ēṟpaṭṭatu. itil 357 iyakkam

kaittaṟikaḷ 1400 mukāmaik kaittaṟi necavāḷar kūṭṭuṟavu amaippiṉ kīḻ ceyalpaṭṭaṉa. aracu necavāḷar mēmpāṭṭiṟkāka āṇṭoṉ ṟukku urupā 154 ilakkam otukkiyatu. kuṭicait toḻilkaḷ 
kuṭicait toḻil mēmpāṭṭiṟkāka aracu kuṭicait toḻilkaḷ mēmpāṭṭuk kaḻakam" oṉṟai 1960-l toṭaṅkiyatu. itaṉ . vaḻi pala āyiram vaḷarppura makkaḷukku vēlaivāyppukaḷ kiṭaittaṉa. calaip pōkkuvarattu 
poruḷātāra vaḷarccikku mikavum payaṉpaṭuvatu cālappōkku varattu ākum, karppuraṅkaḷaiyum, nakarppuraṅkaḷaiyum iṇaikkavum, cantaikaḷai vaḷarkkavum cālaip pōkkuvarattu iṉṟiyamaiyāta tākiṟatu. itaṉ mēmpāṭṭiṟkāka aracu "tamiḻnāṭu cālaip pōkku varattukkaḻakam" eṉṟa niṟuvaṉattai ēṟpaṭuttiyatu. kāmarācar āṭcik kālattil tamiḻakattil mottam 170ilakkam kilōmīṭṭar tūrattiṟku tamiḻnāṭu pēruntukaḷ ōṭiṉa. toḻiṟ caṅkaṅkaḷ 
toḻiṟ vaḷarcci yeṉpatu toḻiṟcālaikaḷiṉ vaḷarcci eṉpataik kaṇṭōm. iyantirattāl avai iyakkappaṭṭālum, toḻilāḷarkaḷē itaṉai iyakkiṉar. eṉavē, toḻiṟpēṭṭai kaḷaippōlavē toḻilāḷar kuṭiyiruppukaḷum perukiṉa. avarkaḷiṉ muṉṉēṟṟattiṟkeṉa mutalāḷikaḷai allatu aracaik kēṭṭuppeṟat toḻiṟcaṅkaṅkaḷ ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. avai toḻilāḷar oṟṟumai, muṉṉēṟ ṟam mutaliyavaṟṟōṭu nāṭṭuṭaimaivaḷam, muṉṉēṟṟam, orumaippāṭ ṭiṟkum pāṭupaṭṭaṉa. toḻiṟcālaic caṭṭaṅkaḷ palavum tōṉṟiṉa. 
tamiḻ nāṭṭil toḻiṟcaṅkaṅkaḷiṉ tantaiyeṉap pōṟṟappaṭu pavarkaḷ eṉem, cōci, pipi, vāṭiyā, ēci.ci. antōṉippiḷḷai , tiru.vi.ka, em.ci. irācā mutaliyōrāvar. tamiḻnāṭu aḷavilum, tēciya aḷavilum, ulaka nāṭukaḷ aḷavilum toḻilāḷar cikkalkaḷaip pēcat tāḷikaikaḷum, araciyal kaṭcikaḷumirukkiṉṟaṉa. toḻilāḷarē toḻiṟ caṅkat talaivarāka iruppatē nalam. itu araciyalukku appāṟpaṭṭu iruttal mika, mikanalam. piṟpaṭṭōr mēmpāṭu 
piṟpaṭṭōr makkaḷ tokaiyaik kaṇakkiṭṭu, avarkaḷukkuḷ ḷēyē mikavum piṟpaṭṭōraik kaṇṭaṟintu avarkaḷukkuc calukai kaḷaik koṭuttavar kāmarācar.pērāyak kaṭci āṭcikkuppiṉ ēṟpaṭṭa ti.mu.ka. āṭci kālattil mu.karuṇāniti itaṟkeṉa oru āṇaiyattai amartti ataṉ parinturaikaḷaic ceyalpaṭutti, piṟpaṭṭōr nalattuṟai yaic caṭṭappaṭi amaittār.

tāynilavaralāṟu 
kāmarācar āṭciyil tāḻttappaṭṭōrukkum, piṟpaṭṭōruk kum, mikavum piṟpaṭṭōrukkum vēṟupāṭiṉṟik kalvic calukaikaḷum, aracuppaṇikaḷil paṭippu vayatuvarampu mutaliyavaṟṟilum camamākavē naṉmai ēṟpaṭṭatu. piṟpaṭṭōril 54 cātiyiṉar mikavum piṟpaṭṭōr (Most Backward) eṉak kaṇṭaṟintu taṉic calukaikaḷ vaḻaṅkappaṭṭaṉa. ivarkaḷil āṅkila aracāl kuṟṟapparamparaiyiṉar (Criminal Tribes) eṉak kuṟikkappaṭṭavarkaḷaiyum aracuppaṇikaḷil muḻumaiyāka iṭampeṟac ceytār, mīṉavar nala mēmpāṭu 
mīṉavarkaḷ mikavum piṉtaṅkiyavarkaḷ. avarkaḷiṉ mēmpāṭṭiṟ kākkuṟainta vilaiyil 'pāplō muṟai iyantirap paṭakukaḷ' vāṅkit tarappaṭṭaṉa; itaip pōlavē nailāṉ mīṉpiṭi valaikaḷum malivu vilaiyil koṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. ittōṭu urupā 36 ilakkam niti avarkaḷiṉ poruḷātāra mēmpāṭṭiṟkāka otukkappaṭṭatu. nākap paṭṭiṉam, kaṭalūr, irāyapuram, līpuram ākiya tuṟaimukaṅkaḷil mīṉ tuṟaikaḷ kaṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. piṭitta mīṉkaḷaik keṭṭuppōkāmal pātukāttu vaikka āṅkāṅkē kuḷircātaṉa aṟaikaḷ kaṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. 
aracitaḻpeṟāta aracu ūḻiyar mēmpāṭu (N.G.0) 
ivarkaḷiṉ kuḻantaikaḷukkup paḷḷi iṟutippaṭippuvaraik kaṭṭaṇa millāmal paṭikkavaḻi ceyyappaṭṭatu. ivvūḻiyarkaḷukkut tiṅkaḷūtiyam 172 rūpāy atikarittut tarappaṭṭatu: ilavaca maruttuva utavi aḷikkappaṭṭatu. kāvalkārarkaḷukku (Police) kōṭi rupāyil 3500 vīṭukaḷ kaṭṭittarappaṭṭaṉa. potuvāka aracu ūḻiyarukku vaḻiya āṇaik kuḻuviṉ parinturaippaṭi ūtiya uyarvum, ōyvu ūtiyamum aḷikkappaṭṭaṉa. cēri cīramaipput tiṭṭam virivupaṭuttutal

ivaruṭaiya kālattil mūṉṟāvatu aintāṇṭu tiṭṭam (1961-66) ceyalpāṭṭiṟku vantatu. 
kalviyil tamiḻnāṭu ciṟanta muṉṉēṟṟamaṭaintatu. kalvittuṟai ivariṭamē iruntatu. varumāṉa uccavarampu illāmal ellā māṇavarukkum paḷḷi iṟuti vakuppuvarai ilavacak kalviyaḷittār, kāmarācar kālattil tara makkaḷ tokaiyuḷḷa ūrkaḷil uyarnilaip paḷḷikaḷ toṭaṅkappaṭṭaṉa. paktavatcalam 300 makkaḷ tokaiyuḷḷa ūrkaḷilellām uyarnilaip paḷḷikaḷait toṭaṅkiṉār. toḻil nuṭpappaḷḷikaḷai atika eṇṇikkaiyil toṭaṅkiṉār. cēlattil putiya poṟiyiyal kallūriyait toṭaṅkiṉār. tiruccirāppaḷḷiyil vaṭṭārap poṟiyiyal kallūri toṭaṅkappaṭṭatu. 
mūṉṟukaḷ cuṟṟu vaṭṭārattiṟku oru toṭakkappaḷḷiyait toṭaṅkiya kāmarācar aṭiccuvaṭṭaip piṉpaṟṟi ivar orukal cuṟṟu vaṭṭārattiṟku oru toṭakkappaḷḷiyait toṭaṅkiṉār. avaṟṟil 41 āciriyarkaḷaip putitāka amarttiṉār. āciriyaikaḷukku ūrppuṟaṅkaḷil 750 kuṭiyiruppukaḷaik kaṭṭiṉār. āciriyarkaḷiṉ ōyvu peṟum akavaiyai 55liruntu 58 āka aracu ūḻiyarkaḷukku iruppataip pōlavē uyarttiṉār. putitāka aintu aracuk kalaik kallūrikaḷait toṭaṅkiṉār, āṟu taṉiyār kalluārikaḷait toṭaṅka aṉumati aḷittār. mālai nērak kalluārikaḷum toṭaṅkappaṭṭaṉa. ceṅkalpaṭṭu, kōvai, tirunelvēli ākiya iṭaṅkaḷil putiya maruttuvak kalluārikaḷ toṭaṅkappaṭṭaṉa, ti.2. 
16-l maturaip palkalaikkaḻakam toṭaṅkappaṭṭatu. 
ēṉ ciṟappiḻantu vīḻntār? kāmarācar aṭiyoṟṟik kalvi vaḷarcciyil cātaṉaip paṭaitta paktavatcalam ēṉ makkaḷāl paḻikkap paṭṭār? pērāyak kaṭciyiṉ āṭciyē aṭiccuvaṭu aṟṟuppōkak karaṇiyamāka raṉ amaintār? avar ceyta orē tavaṟu intiyaik kaṭṭāyap pāṭamākki 1964-l māṇavariṭaiyēyum, makkaḷiṭaiyēyum veṟuppait tēṭikkoṇṭatēyākum.

inti, nāṭāḷum maṉṟattil orē oru vākku atikam peṟṟu āṭci moḻic caṭṭattāl āṭci moḻiyāṉatu. āṉāl, paktavatcalam ataṉaic cuṭṭāyappāṭamākkiṉār. itaṉai etirttut tamiḻakattil naṭanta moḻippōrāl caṭṭam, oḻuṅku keṭṭatu. naṭuvaṇ āṭciyil amaiccarkaḷāyirunta ci. cuppiramaṇiyam, ōvi, aḻakēcaṉ ākiyōrum patavi tuṟantaṉar. tantai periyāriṉ tirāviṭak kaḻakamum, aṇṇāviṉ tirāviṭa muṉṉēṟṟak kaḻakamum, oṭṭumotta māṇavarkaḷum, potu makkaḷum, moḻippōril iṟaṅkiṉar. aṇṭai mānilaṅkaḷilum moḻippōr paraviyatu. aricippaṟṟākkuṟai maṟupuṟam makkaḷai vāṭṭiyatu. intac cūḻalil 19 ām āṇṭu pippiravariyil potut tērtal vantatu. itil timuka, kūṭṭaṇi veṟṟip peṟṟu amaiccaravai amaittatu, muṭivurai

kā. na. aṇṇāturai (ci.eṉ. aṇṇāturai) (6.3.1969) 
1957-l naṭanta aintāvatu potuttērtalil tirāviṭa muṉṉēṟṟak kaḻakam veṟṟip peṟṟu āṭci amaittatu. kā.na. aṇṇāturai mutalva rāṉār. iraṇṭu āṇṭukaḷukkup piṉ avar maṟaintār. avar āṇṭa

aḻaikkap peṟṟatu. aṇṇā itaṉai māṟṟit tamiḻ nāṭu' eṉṟu peyariṭṭār, tamiḻ kūṟum nallulakam ki.pi. 20 ām nūṟṟāṇṭiltāṉ ''tamiḻ nāṭu' eṉap peyar peṟṟatu! 
1955 ām āṇṭil koṇṭu varappaṭṭa intuttirumaṇac caṭṭam" eṉpatait tirutti cuyamariyātait tirumaṇac caṭṭam yeṉa veḷiyiṭṭār. tantai periyārāl toṭaṅkappaṭṭa cuyamariyātai iyakkam 1925-liruntu naṭatti vaittat "taṉmāṉat tirumaṇaṅkaḷum" itaṉpaṭi cellupaṭiyāyiṉa. ittirumaṇattil aiyartiṭaiyātu. ammi mitittal, aruntatippārttal, tīvalam varutal mutaliya caṭaṅkukaḷ kiṭaiyātu. tāli kaṭṭalām, allatu mōtiram māṟṟikkoḷḷalām. allatu mālai māṟṟikkoḷḷalām. ituvum intut tirumaṇattaip pōlavē caṭṭappaṭi cellum. tēvaippaṭṭāl pativu ceytu koḷḷalām. kulam, kōttiram, cāti kaṭṭut tiṭṭaṅkaḷ itil illai, varuṇācarumappaṭi pirāmaṇaṉ uyarntavaṉ. avaṉ tāliyeṭuttuk koṭuttāltāṉ tirumaṇam puṉitamāṉatākum eṉṟa cāti ēṟṟattāḻvai oḻippatē it tirumaṇattiṉ nōkkameṉṟār. aṇṇā itaṉai ''vāḻkkai oppantam'' eṉakkūṟi āṇum, peṇṇum camameṉap paṟaicāṟṟiṉār. taṅkap patakkam vaḻaṅkutal 
oru āti tirāviṭa maṇamakaṉ allatu maṇamakaḷ vēṟṟuccāti maṇamakaṉ allatu maṇamakaḷait tirumaṇam ceytu koṇṭāl inta maṇamakkaḷukku aracu taṅkappatakkam vaḻaṅkum tiṭṭattai aṟimukap paṭuttiṉār. ittiṭṭattil 1968 mutal 1976-kkuṭpaṭṭa kālak kaṭṭattil 417 tampatikaḷ taṅkappatakkam peṟṟārkaḷ. 
uruvavaḻipāṭu intuc camayattiṉ aṭippaṭaik kōṭpāṭā kum. camayam taṉippaṭṭōriṉ aṭippaṭai urimaiyākum. āṉāl, aracu aluvalakaṅkaḷil intukkaḷ allātavarkaḷum paṇiyāṟṟuvatāl uruva vaḻippāṭṭil nampikkaiyillātavarkaḷaiyum nampikkaik koḷḷu māṟu ceyvataip pōl aracu aluvalakaṅkaḷil intuk kaṭavuḷkaḷiṉ uruvappaṭaṅkaḷai vaittu vaḻipaṭuvatākum. eṉavē, ittakaiya uruvap paṭaṅkaḷai aracu aluvalakaṅkaḷiliruntu akaṟṟi viṭavēṇṭumeṉa aṇṇā āṇaiyoṉṟaip pōṭṭār. 
intiyai aṭiyōṭu oḻittu, inti āciriyarkaḷai māṟṟup paṇiyil amarttiṉār.

aṉṟē mu. karuṇānitiyai mutalamaiccarākkiṉar.

tāynila varalāṟu 
mu. karuṇāniti (10.2.1969 - 31.1.1976) cātaṉaikaḷ a) kumukāya muṉṉēṟṟattil: 1. tīṇṭāmai oḻippukkuṟṟac caṭṭam, civil urikamap pātukāppuccaṭṭam 
(Protection of Civil Rights Act) ākiya tīṇṭāmaikku etirāṉa caṭṭaṅkaḷaic ceyalpaṭuttit tīṇṭāmaikkuṟṟaṅkaḷaic ceyvōr, tūṇṭuvōr, uṭantaiyāka iruppōr mutaliyōraik karuṇāniti taṇṭittār. itaṟkeṉa 'paṟakkum paṭaikaḷ' eṉum kāval paṭaikaḷai amarttit tīṇṭāmaik kuṟṟam naṭakkum iṭaṅkaḷukkum, naṭakka mukāntiramuḷḷa iṭaṅkaḷukkum aṉuppik kuṟṟavāḷikaḷait taṇṭit tum, kuṟṟam nikaḻāmal taṭukkavum ceytār, pātikkappaṭṭavarukku vaḻakkaṟiñarkaḷai amartti vaḻakkāṭa utaviṉār. 2 intu aṟanilaiyac caṭṭattait tirutti, aṉaittuc cātiyiṉarum arccakarākum caṭṭa muṉvaraivu (1970) 
arccakar patavi cāti aṭippaṭaiyil amaintatu. pirāmaṇaṉ tavira maṟṟa cātiyiṉar ip patavikku varamuṭiyātu. kaṭavuḷ vīṭṭil kōyilil cātikūṭātu eṉpatu timuka viṉ kōṭpāṭu. eṉavē, pūcai vitimuṟaikaḷaik kaṟṟut tēṟum evarum arccakarāka varalāmeṉṉum nōkkattil " aṉaittuc cātiyiṉarum arccakarākalām" eṉṟa caṭṭa muṉvaraivaikkaruṇānitikkoṇṭuvaramuṉaintār. itaṟkāka 1959 ām āṇṭu intuc camaya aṟanilaiyac caṭṭattil uḷḷa vitikaḷ55, 56 ākiya iraṇṭaiyum tirutti allatu māṟṟi amaikka vēṇṭum eṉa muṭivu ceytār. 
viti 55-l intu camaya aṟanilaiya aṟakkaṭṭaḷaiyiṉpaṭi, intuk kōyilkaḷ, maṭaṅkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟil paṇiyāṟṟuvōr vāḻaiyaṭi vāḻaiyāka "'takappaṉukkuppiṉ makaṉ" eṉṟa muṟaiyil paṇi ceyyavēṇṭum. ip paṇikaḷil āṭkaḷai amarttum poṟuppu aṟaṅkāvalarkaḷukkē uṇṭu. itil aracu talaiyiṭak kūṭātu." eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu; vitika-l arccakar pataviyum ittakaiyatē. appatavi veṟṟiṭamāki viṭṭālum, vāricu illāmal pōyviṭṭālum ataṉai nirappa vēṇṭiya poṟuppu aṟaṅkāvalaruṭaiyatu. kuṟṟaṅkuṟaikaḷēṟ paṭṭālum kuṟṟavāḷiyait arccakaṉai taṇṭippatu tuṇai āṇaiyar poṟuppu. itil aracu talaiyiṭa urimai illai eṉat tiṭṭa vaṭṭamākak kūṟappaṭṭu uḷḷatāl, ic caṭṭa vitikaḷai māṟṟiṉāl vēṇṭiya takutikaḷaiyuṭaiyavarai arccakarāka amartta muṭiyum eṉpatai yuṇarnta karuṇāniti, mutalil ic caṭṭattait tiruttum caṭṭa muṉvaraivaic caṭṭamaṉṟattil 1970 ām āṇṭu koṇṭuvantār. 
āṇaiyarperpavāḷiyait (arccaṭaiyatu. kuṟṟaṅku

- ''pala āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē āti - tirāviṭarkaḷiṉ kōyil nuḻaivu ēṟpaṭṭu viṭṭatu. 1950 ām āṇṭu koṇṭuvarappaṭṭa araciyal caṭṭattil evarukkum enta oru paṇiyum avaruṭaiya cātiyiṉ peyarāl maṟukkamuṭiyātu eṉṟum, araciyal caṭṭaviti 7l tīṇṭāmai oḻikkappaṭṭatu eṉṟum kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatāl āṇṭavaṉ muṉ aṉaivarum camam eṉṟa vitiyai nilaināṭṭavē intac caṭṭattaik koṇṭuvarukiṟōm'' eṉṟu karuṇāniti kūṟiṉār. caṭṭam niṟaivēṟi yatu. kuṭiyaracut talaivar opputalukkāka aṉuppappaṭṭatu. āṉāl, arccakarkaḷ, marapukaḷukkum, ākamatarumaṅkaḷukkum etirāka uḷḷatāl iccaṭṭattai nirākarikkak kōri tilli ucca nayaṉmai maṉṟattil vaḻakkut toṭuttaṉar. itaṉai ēṟṟu, ucca nayaṉmai maṉṟam ic caṭṭattai nirākarittatu. timuka aracu tam muyaṟciyil tōṟṟatu. 3. āti-tirāviṭarkaḷaik kōyil aṟaṅkāvalarkaḷākkutal: 
āti - tirāviṭar kōyil nuḻaivu ēṟkaṉavē nikaḻntuviṭṭatu. araciyal caṭṭappaṭi tīṇṭāmai illai. eṉavē, aṟaṅkāvalarkaḷāka avarkaḷai niyamippatu ti.mu.ka. aracukku eḷitākiviṭṭatu. mutalil 49 āti tirāviṭarkaḷ periya kōyilkaḷiṉ aṟaṅkāvalarkaḷāka amarttappaṭṭaṉar. 4. piṟpaṭṭōr mēmpāṭu: 
piṟpaṭṭōr (backward Class) yeṉṟa collum ataṟkāṉa viḷakka uraiyum mutalil intiya araciyal caṭṭattil illai , 1951-l intiya araciyal caṭṭam mutaṉmuṟaiyāka tiruttam ceytapiṉtāṉ intiya araciyal caṭṭam15 (4) 16 (4) vitikaḷil cērkkappaṭṭatu. ataṟkuc cariyāṉa poruḷum, viḷakka uraiyum tantavar intiya araciyal caṭṭac ciṟpi ṭākṭar.pi. ār. ampētkār avarkaḷē! 
''kumukāya nilaiyilum, kalviyilum piṉtaṅkiyavarkaḷ piṟpaṭṭava rāvar" eṉpatu. avar tanta viḷakkamākum. araciyal caṭṭattil iṭam peṟṟa ivarkaḷukku mānila aracukaḷ taṉippaṭṭa muṟaiyil muṉṉēṟṟat tiṟkāṉa ēntukaḷaic ceyyalāmeṉṟatāl piṟpaṭṭac cātikaḷiṉ paṭṭiyalai tamiḻnāṭu aracu tayārittatu. itaṟkākac caṭṭanātaṉ āṇaikkuḻuvai1971-l ēṟpaṭutti piṟpaṭṭa cātikaḷ evai evaiyeṉak kaṇṭaṟintu, avaṟṟiṉ mēmpāṭṭiṟkāka ti.mu.ka. aracupāṭupaṭṭatu. tamiḻ nāṭṭiṉ motta makkaḷtokaiyil 54 viḻukkāṭu piṟpaṭṭōr iruppatākak kaṇṭaṟiyappaṭṭatu. 
ucca nayaṉmai maṉṟattiṉ tīrppuppaṭi motta otukkīṭu 50 viḻukkāṭṭiṟku atikamāka irukkakkūṭātu eṉpatai maṉatiṟkoṇṭu ' 
mu. karuṇāniti piṟpaṭṭōrukku 25-liruntu 32 catavikitamum, tāḻttap paṭṭōrukku 18 catavikitamumāka motta otukkīṭu 50 catavikitam irukkumpaṭic ceytār,

tāynila varalāṟu 
caṭṭanātaṉ āṇaik kuḻuviṉ parinturaiyil piṟpaṭṭōril ''micuvum piṟpaṭṭōr" (Most Backward) tōrāyamāka 25 viḻukkāṭu, uḷḷatākak kūṟappaṭṭiruntatu. itaṉaṭippaṭaiyil ti.mu.ka., āṭcik kālattil 1968-l mikavum piṟpaṭṭōr [Most Backward Class) paṭṭiyal tayārikkappaṭṭatu. mēlum pala cātikaḷ piṟpaṭṭōr paṭṭiyalil cērkkappaṭṭaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ piṟpaṭṭa cātikaḷiliruntu iculām. kiṟittuvam māṟiya cātikaḷum piṟpaṭṭavarkaḷākavē kaṇakkiṭap paṭṭaṉar. eṭuttukkāṭṭāka nāṭār cātiyiliruntu kiṟittuvam māṟiyavar kaḷum, intu nāṭārkaḷum piṟpaṭṭavarākavē koḷḷappaṭṭaṉar. āṉāl, tāḻttappaṭṭa cātiyiliruntu kiṟittuvam māṟiyōr piṟpaṭṭa varākavē atāvatu muṉṉēṟiyavarkaḷākak koḷḷappaṭṭaṉar. itu ēṉ eṉṟu puriyavillai . 
mu. karuṇāniti ti.mu.ka. āṭciyai amaittavuṭaṉēyē 1959-l ''piṟpaṭṭōr nalattuṟaiyait tōṟṟuvittār. nayaṉmaik kaṭci 1920-l āṭcikku varuvataṟku muṉpē piraṭṭāṉiyar toḻilāḷar nalat tuṟaiyait (Labour #WelfarE [EpartiTIEnt) tōṟṟuvittaṉar. omantarar irāmacāmi reṭṭiyār mutalvarāṉavuṭaṉ 1947-lēyē at tuṟaiyai aricaṉa nalattuṟai toḻilāḷar nalattuṟaiyeṉa iraṇṭākap pirittār. 1947-liruntu karuṇāniti mutalvarākum 1969 ām āṇṭuvarai aricaṉa nalattuṟaiyāl piṟpaṭṭōr nalamum kavaṉikkappaṭṭu vantatu. āṉāl, karuṇāniti āṭcikku vantavuṭaṉ aricaṉa nalattuṟaiyiliruntu pirittu ''piṟpaṭṭōr nalattuṟai' eṉum putiya tuṟaiyai ēṟpaṭutti, ataṟkuk kālañceṉṟa eṉ.vi.naṭarācaṉai amaiccarākkiṉār. ittuṟaikkup peruntokaiyai otukki araciṉ cellakkuḻantaiyāka ataṉaip pēṇikkāttār. kuṟippākap piṟpaṭṭōr kalvi mēmpāṭṭiṟkāka mikap periya tokai otukkappaṭṭatu. atu māṇavariṉ utavittokaikkum, viṭutikaḷukkum celaviṭappaṭṭatu. 5. ciṟītaṉa tiruttac caṭṭam(1958) 
oru peṇṇait tirumaṇam ceyyumpōtu varataṭcaṉai vāṅku vatum kēṭpatum kuṟṟameṉappaṭṭaṉa. maṇamakaṉ, maṇamakaḷ peṟṟōr allatu uṟaviṉariṭamiruntu nēraṭiyākavō allatu maṟaimukamākavō ciṟītaṉam allatu varataṭcaṉai vāṅkiṉāl avarkaḷ āṟu tiṅkaḷ mutal iraṇṭu āṇṭukaḷ varai ciṟai taṇṭaṉaip peṟuvar eṉpatu ic caṭṭattiṉ karupporuḷākum. 
mēlum, kuṟṟaviyal caṭṭam 174-l 3iṉpaṭi oru peṇ tirumaṇa māki ēḻu āṇṭukkuḷ varataṭcaṇaik koṭumaiyāl iṟantupaṭṭāl avaḷ cāvukkuk karaṇiyamāṉavarkaḷ taṇṭaṉaikkuriyavarāvar. 6. karukkalaippuc caṭṭam (1971)

āti - tirāviṭap paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷ uṇavu, uṭai, uṟaiviṭam ākiya aṭippaṭait tēvaikaḷaik kūṭap peṟāmal vāḻntu vantaṉar, nakarppuṟaṅkaḷil vāḻnta am makkaḷ paḷḷamāṉa, cērum, cakatiyumuḷḷa iṭaṅkaḷil kuṭicaikaḷil vāḻntaṉar. inta vāḻviṭaṅkaḷaiyum, potuvāka tamiḻnāṭu muḻuvatilumuḷḷa āti tirāviṭarkaḷiṉ vāḻ viṭaṅkaḷaiyum mēmpaṭuttavē "tamiḻnāṭu āti tirāviṭar vīṭṭuvacati vāriyam"' 1974-l toṭaṅkappaṭṭatu. itaṟku naṭuvaṇ āṭciyum peruntokaiyai māṉiyamāka vaḻaṅki vantatu. 
nalla terukkaḷum, kāṟṟōṭṭamum, veḷiccamum uḷḷa tīppiṭikkāta cimeṇṭut takaṭukaḷāl ivai cuṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. kuṭinīrk kuḻāykaḷum teru viḷakkukaḷum, kaḻippiṭaṅkaḷum amaittuk koṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. 
1971-l kuṭicai mēmpāṭṭuttiṭṭam (Slurns improvement Scheme) koṇṭu varappaṭṭatu. ataṟkākat tamiḻnāṭu kuṭicaippakuti mēmpāṭu maṟṟum kuṭicaippakuti oḻippuccaṭṭam 1977-l(The Tamil Nadu Slurn Ar As IrmḻaOVErmerit and cleararICE Act of 1971]koṇṭuvarappaṭṭa tu. ip pakutiyil vāḻntavaril ēṟattāḻa 8 viḻukkāṭṭiṉar iculāmiyar 6 viḻukkāṭṭiṉar kiṟittuvarkaḷ maṟṟum itara camayattiṉarāvar; mītamuḷḷa varkaḷil 80 viḻukkāṭṭiṉar āti tirāviṭap paḻaṅkuṭiyiṉar; 12 viḻukkāṭṭiṉar cāti intukkaḷeṉak kaṇakkiṭappaṭṭaṉar.

ti.mu.ka. āṭcik kālattil ē.vi.pi. ācaittampiyiṉ talaimaiyil oru kuḻu amaikkappaṭṭu, piccaikkārar cikkalkaḷaik kaḷaintu, maṟuvāḻvu aḷittu, irappōrē illai eṉumāṟu eppaṭic ceyalpaṭa lāmeṉa āynturaikkumāṟu kēṭṭuk koḷḷappaṭṭatu. intakkuḻu, piccaikkārarait " tiṭamāṉa uṭal nilaiyuḷḷavarkaḷ" eṉṟum, uṭal nalintavarum, vayatāṉavarumāṉavarkaḷ" eṉṟum, nōyāl pātikkap paṭṭōr, iḷaṅkuṟṟavāḷikaḷ" eṉṟum mūvakaiyākap pirittu, taṉit taṉiyē cikkalkaḷai āyntatu. āyiṉum, potuvāka tamiḻakattil piccai yeṭukkum nilaikkut taḷḷappaṭuvatiṉ karaṇiyaṅkaḷaiyum āyn turaittatu. iyaṟkaik kēṭukaḷālēṟpaṭum pañcam, paṭṭiṉi mutaliya vaṟṟālum, vēlaiyillāttālum kuṭumpam keṭṭuc cīraḻintu viṭuvatā lum, allatu kuṭumpattilēṟpaṭum caṇṭai caccaravukaḷālum, kaṇavaṉ kaiviṭuvatāl maṉaiviyum, kuḻantaikaḷum, piḷḷaikaḷum kaiviṭuvatāl mūppaṭainta peṟṟōrkaḷum, tīrāta nōyāl kaiviṭappaṭṭavarum, ulakait tuṟantavarkaḷum, kātalil tōlviyuṟṟavarkaḷum piccaiyeṭuk 
kum nilaikkut taḷḷappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. eṉavē, piccaikkārarkaḷ kumukāyap , piraccaṉaiyākavum, tēciyap piraccaṉaiyākavum uḷḷaṉar. ivarkaḷai oḻippatē araciṉ kaṭamaiyeṉa ācaittampikkuḻu kūṟiyatu. tiṭakāttira māṉapiccaikkārarukkeṉa māṟṟu maṟuvāḻvu maiyaṅkaḷai ēṟpaṭutti, vēlaikoṭuttum, maṟṟavarukku vēṇṭiya ēntukaḷaic ceytum aracu kākka vēṇṭum eṉavum ikkuḻu parinturaittatu.

māntaṉai māntaṉ vaṇṭiyil vaittu iḻuttuc cellum kaivaṇṭi allatu naravaṇṭi (Hand Pulling Rickhaw) makkaḷāṭciyil irukkakkūṭātu eṉṟa aṭippaṭaiyil itaṉait timuka aracu oḻittatu. ivvāṟu 2000 naravaṇṭikaḷai oḻittu, attoḻilāḷarkaḷukku tuvic cakkara vaṇṭikaḷai (Cycle Rickhaws) aracu ilavayamākak koṭuttatu. itaṟkākak koṭaimaṉam paṭaittōrkaḷum urupā 25 ilakkam koṭuttaṉar. perumpālāṉavarkaḷ naravaṉcaṭikaḷaic contakkārariṭam miruntu vāṭakaikkup peṟṟē iḻuttu, campātittu vāḻntaṉar. attakaiya contakkārarukkut talā urupā 200 aracu iḻappāṭāka vaḻaṅkiyatu. 
ivvāṟu intiyāvilēyē piccaikkārarai oḻittatum, naravaṇṭikaḷai oḻittatum tamiḻnāṭutāṉ. 10. ēḻai, ētilikarukku ilavaca maruttuva utavikaḷ 
a) kaṇ cikiccai mukāmkaḷ 
ūrppuraṅkaḷil mukāmkaḷai amaittu ēḻai makkaḷukku ilavaya kaṇ cikiccaikaḷai aḷippatōṭu, kaṇ kaṇṇāṭikaḷaiyum avarka ḷukku vaḻaṅkum tiṭṭattai 1972-l toṭaṅkiṉar. ittakaiya tiṭṭattaik kaṇṇoḷi vaḻaṅkum tiṭṭam' eṉṟaṉar. ittakaiya tiṭṭam pērāyak kaṭciyiṉ āṭcik kālattilumiruntatu. āṉāl timuka, āṭciyil muṭukkamākkappaṭṭu, makkaḷiṭaiyē varavēṟkappaṭṭatu. itaṟkākap potumakkaḷē muṉ vantu oṉpatu ilakkam urupā naṉkoṭaiyum aḷittaṉar. itil aintu ilakkam urupāyai emēem.

āēḻaikaḷ taṉ muyaṟcip paṇikaḷait toṭaṅkak kaṭaṉ vaḻaṅkutal:--

kāriyaṅkaḷil payaṉpaṭuttappaṭutal vēṇṭum. 2. araciyal caṭṭattiṉ 46vatu 347vatu pirivukaḷukku uḷaṟiṉṟi aluval 
muṟai kāriyaṅkaḷai āṅkila muṟaiyil toṭarntu payaṉpaṭutta vēṇṭum 3. tamiḻil veḷiyiṭa vēṇṭiyavaṟṟukku tamiḻaiyē payaṉpaṭuttalām. 4. caṭṭamūlaṅkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟait tamiḻilēyē payaṉpaṭuttalām, 
itaṟkāṉa aṟivikkaiyai mānila aracu muṉkūṭṭiyē veḷiyiṭa vēṇṭum. caṭṭa mūlaṅkaḷāvaṉa: 
tamiḻnāṭu caṭṭamaṉṟattil koṇṭu varappeṟum caṭṭamuṉ varaivukaḷ, allatu caṭṭat tiruttaṅkaḷ intiya araciyal caṭṭattiṉ kīḻp pēraṟikkaiyāka pirakaṭaṉam veḷiyiṭappeṟum avacara kālac caṭṭaṅkaḷ, naṭuvaṇ āṭciyāl veḷiyiṭappaṭum caṭṭam, mānila aracāl iyaṟṟappaṭṭa caṭṭam mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ kīḻ mānila aracāl piṟappikkappaṭum āṇaikaḷ vitikaḷ, oḻuṅkumuṟai vitikaḷ, tuṇai vitikaḷ ākiyavaṟṟait tamiḻilēyē veḷiyiṭal vēṇṭum; enta oru aṟivikkaiyum, iru avaikaḷilum vaittu vivātittu muṭivu kāṇa vēṇṭum, 
ivvāṟu ac caṭṭattil kaṇmūṭittaṉamākat tamiḻai maṭṭumē aluvalakak kāriyaṅkaḷil ceyalpaṭuttāmal āṅkilattai vēṇṭiya iṭattil payaṉpaṭutta vaḻi ceyyum vakaiyum kūṟappeṟṟatu. itaṉai viraivupaṭutti, āṅkilattai nīkkit tamiḻai maṭṭumē payaṉpaṭuttum. aṭuttak kaṭṭappaṇiyil aṭuttu vanta tirāviṭa muṉṉēṟṟak kaḻaka āṭci muṭukkamāyc ceyalpaṭṭatu. 
1947-l aṇṇā mutalvarāṉavuṭaṉ paḷḷikaḷilirunta inti āciriyarkaḷukku māṟṟup paṇikaḷ aḷittu intiyaip paḷḷikaḷi liruntu nīkkiṉār. āṭcimoḻi tamiḻ maṭṭumēyeṉa aṟivittār. āṭci moḻi vaḷarccikkāka 1968- l ka tamiḻ vaḷarccittuṟai" eṉṟa oru taṇittuṟaiyai ēṟpaṭutti, atai muḻuvaḷarcciyaṭainta tuṟaipōl ceyalpaṭuvataṟku vēṇṭiya ellā ēntukaḷaiyum ceytār; 1857 mutal 1957varaiyil oru nūṟṟāṇṭu kālattil veḷiyāṉa tamiḻ nūlkaḷait tokuttu ''accāṉa tamiḻ nūlkaḷiṉ paṭṭiyal" veḷiyiṭappaṭṭatu.

tāynilavaralāṟu 
1971-l "āṭcic col akaramutali'' mūṉṟām patippāka veḷiyiṭap paṭṭatu. 
1970 ām āṇṭu kūṭiya tamiḻ vaḷarcci ārāycci maṉṟam' tamiḻ nāṭṭiṉ varalāṟṟai eḻuti veḷiyiṭa muṭivu ceytatu. ataṟkut talaimai tāṅkiya mutalvar mu.karuṇāniti tamiḻaka varalāṟṟai eḻuta oru kuḻuvai amaittār. ak kuḻuvukkut tamiḻaṟiñar mu. varatarācaṉār talaivarākkappaṭṭār. ak kuḻu, varalāṟṟu aṟiñarkaḷiṭamiruntu 
āyvuk kaṭṭuraikaḷaip peṟṟut tamiḻaka varalāṟṟait tolpaḻaṅkālam mutal iṉṟu varai pala maṭalaṅkaḷāka veḷiyiṭṭatu. kaṭṭuraiyāḷarkaḷ koṭukkum kaṭṭuraikaḷai ik kuḻu āyntapiṉ avaṟṟai nūlākap patittu veḷiyiṭap patippāciriyarkaḷ nirantarap paṇiyāḷarkaḷāka amarttappaṭṭaṉar. ippaṇi, tamiḻ vaḷarccit tuṟaiyil ceyalpaṭṭatu. 
tolpaḻaṅkālam, caṅkakālam, pallavar - pāṇṭiyar kālam, cōḻap peruvēntar kālam, pāṇṭiyar perum vēntarkālam, mutaliya maṭalaṅkaḷākat tamiḻnāṭṭu varalāṟu' veḷiyiṭappaṭṭatu. 
aṭuttapaṭiyākap paḷḷikaḷukkum, kallūrikaḷukkum tamiḻil pāṭanūlkaḷ veḷiyiṭum tiṭṭamum ceyalpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. ataṟkākat "tamiḻ nāṭṭuppāṭanūl niṟuvaṉam" eṉṟa niṟuvaṉamum ēṟpaṭṭatu. ciṟanta āṅkila nūlkaḷ tamiḻil moḻip peyarkkappaṭṭaṉa. itaip pōlavē, tamiḻil uḷḷa nūlkaḷ āṅkilattilum moḻip peyarkkap paṭṭaṉa. tamiḻmoḻiyai aṟiyātār tamiḻaik kaṟṟuṇarumpaṭi ēntukaḷ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. piṟa mānilaṅkaḷiluḷḷa palkalaikkaḻakaṅkaḷil tami ḻukkāka tuṟai ēṟpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. āṇṭutōṟum ciṟanta nūlkaḷukkup paricu koṭuttu eḻuttāḷarkaḷ ūkkuvikkappaṭṭaṉar. itait tavira ciṟanta āyvu nūlkaḷaip paṭaippavarkaḷukkup paṇa utaviyum ceyyap paṭṭatu. nalivuṟṟat tamiḻaṟiñarkaḷukku ōyvūtiyamum vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatu. 
āṇṭutōṟum talaiciṟanta aṟiñarkaḷukkup pāratitācaṉ virutu, periyār virutu. aṇṇā virutu mutaliyaṉa aḷittu avarkaḷai ūkku vittaṉar. ciṟanta tamiḻppulavar oruvarai "aracavaik kaviñar" eṉum pataviyilamarttit paḻantamiḻar āṭciyai malarac ceytatu ti.mu.ka aracu. payiṟci vakuppukaḷ 
30.11.1957 mutal āṭci moḻit tiṭṭam tamiḻakattil naṭai muṟaikku vantatu. atuvarai āṅkilam terintavarkaḷē aracup paṇikaḷil iruntaṉar. avarkaḷukkut tamiḻ terintirukka vēṇṭumeṉṟa kaṭṭāya millai. eṉavē, āṭcimoḻi tamiḻ' eṉṟavuṭaṉ tamiḻ teriyāta paṇiyāḷarkaḷukkut tamiḻil payiṟci aḷikkavēṇṭiyatāyiṟṟu. 
ceṉṉai mānakarilum, māvaṭṭaṅkaḷilum, tamiḻp payiṟci vakuppukaḷ" naṭattappaṭṭaṉa. ittakaiya payiṟci vakuppukaḷ 1984 ām āṇṭu cūṉ tiṅkaḷil toṭaṅkappaṭṭaṉa. ivai patiṉōru tiṅkaḷkaḷ

naṭantaṉa. payiṟciyiṉ muṭivil tērvum naṭattappaṭṭatu. ittakaiya payiṟci vakuppukaḷ 1964 mutal 177varai toṭarntu naṭantaṉa. 
intiya āṭcippaṇi (I.A.S) intiyak kāvalpaṇi (I.P.S) mutaliya paṇiyāḷarkaḷ tamiḻakattiṟku otukkappaṭumpōtu avarkaḷil tamiḻ teriyātavarkaḷumiruntaṉar. ittakaiyap payiṟciyāl avarkaḷ tamiḻil paṭikkavum, eḻutavum, kōppukaḷaip paṭittu āṇai iṭavum tēṟiṉar. avarkaḷiṉ niruvākap payiṟciyōṭu tamiḻp payiṟcit tiṭṭamum cērkkappaṭṭatu. 
tamiḻvaḷarcci iyakkunar, māvaṭṭa āṭciyar aluvalakaṅkaḷai yum, tuṟait talaimai aluvalakaṅkaḷaiyum, kālavāriyākat tamiḻil āṭci aluvalakaṅkaḷ naṭappataip paricōtittu āyvu ceytār. itaṉ curaṇiyamākat tamiḻ vaḷarccittuṟaiyil taṉi aluvalarkaḷ eṇṇikkai : atikarittatu. eḻuttuc cīrtiruttam 
aluvalakaṅkaḷil tamiḻvaḻi kāriyaṅkaḷai viraintu ceyalpaṭutta vēṇṭi, tamiḻil taṭṭaccu iyantiraṅkaḷ, tamiḻil curukkeḻuttu muṟai mutaliyaṉa naṭaimuṟaikku vantaṉa. tamiḻmoḻiyil uyir, uyirmey, mey mutaliya eḻuttukaḷiṉ eṇṇikkai atikamiruppatālum, avaṟṟiṉ ōcaikaḷukkēṟpak kuṟil neṭilkaḷiruppatālum taṭṭaccu, curukkeḻut tiṟku ottuvarumāṟu eḻuttuc cīrtiruttam ceyya vēṇṭiyatu avaciya māyiṟṟu. itu tamiḻ āṭcimoḻikkuttāṉ eṉṟālum, pulavar perumakkaḷ itaṟkut taṭaiyāka niṉṟaṉar. eṉavē, avarkaḷiṉ karuttu orumaippāṭṭiṉaip peṟuvataṟkāka aracu tamiḻ eḻuttuc cīrtiruttak kuḻu'' oṉṟai ēṟpaṭutti, ataṟku ""makāvittuvāṉ mē.vī. vēṇu kōpālap piḷḷai avarkaḷait talaivarākkiyatu. ik kuḻu tamiḻ vaḷarcci iyakkunar mēṟpārvaiyiṉ kīḻ iyaṅkiyatu. pulavar perumakkaḷ itaṉ uṟuppiṉarkaḷāka iruntaṉar. kuṟukiyakālamē ikkuḻu āyviṉai mēṟkoṇṭatu. kuḻuttalaivar nōyvāypaṭṭamaiyālum, ti.mu.ka. āṭci māṟṟattālum ikkuḻu ceyaliḻantu niṉṟatu. tamiḻ āṭcimoḻi vaḷarcci varalāṟu

akaramutalikaḷ akkālattil nikaṇṭukaḷ eṉappeṟṟaṉa. ki.pi. 8ām nūṟṟāṇṭil uruvākiya nikaṇṭukaḷ iruvakaippaṭum. avai oru collukkāṉa pala poruḷkaḷaik kūṟum akaramutali, oru collukkāṉa etirmaṟaiyaik kūṟum akaramutali yeṉpaṉavākum. oru col palporuḷ akarāti kavitai vaṭivilāṉatu; ituvarai 22 akarātikaḷ itaip pōl uruvākiyuḷḷaṉa. oru collukku etirāṉa coṟkaḷaik kūṟum akarāti urainaṭaiyilāṉatākum. ki.pi. 1732-l vīramāmuṉivar tokutta ittakaiya "catur akarāti" itaṟku vaḻikāṭṭi yākum. tirāviṭa muṉṉēṟṟak kaḻaka āṭciyil 1969-l "centamiḻc coṟ piṟappiyal akara mutavittiṭṭam" toṭaṅkappeṟṟatu. itaṟkeṉa taṉi " akaramutalit tiṭṭa iyakkakam"toṭaṅkappaṭṭatu,

itaṟkut talaivarāka moḻiñāyiṟu tēvanēyap pāvāṇar amarttam ceyyap peṟṟār. 1997 varai akaramutali iyakkakam 'a' mutal 'oḷa' varaiyilāṉa coṟkaḷait tokuttu veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷatu. toṭarntu paṇikaḷ naṭantu varukiṉṟaṉa. āṭcic col akarāti 
āṭciyaic cemmaippaṭuttum nōkkil tamiḻ vaḷarccittuṟai 1957, 1962, 1971, 1983 ākiya āṇṭukaḷil muṟaiyē mutalām, iraṇṭām, muṉṟām, nāṉkām patippukaḷāka āṭcic col akarātiyai veḷi yiṭṭatu. 1990-l aintām patippum veḷivantuḷḷatu. ēṟattāḻa 78 tuṟaikaḷukkāṉa ciṟappuc col tuṇai akarātikaḷum taṉiyē veḷiyiṭappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa. mottattil oṉpatāyiram āṭciccoṟkaḷ ivvakarātiyil iṭampeṟṟuḷḷaṉa. akarātikaḷ ovvoru tuṟaikkum vaḻaṅkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. 
mēlē kūṟappeṟṟa cumār 61 paṇikaḷiṉ paṭṭiyalil tamiḻ moḻiyaic cemmaippaṭutti virivākkum ceyal tiṭṭaṅkaḷ ovvoṉṟum ti.mu.ka. āṭciyil cevvaṉē ceyalpaṭṭatu.

tamiḻiyal āyvukkāka tamiḻaka aracu 1997-1998-l inniṟuvaṉattiṟ kāka urūpāy 90 - ilakkam niti utavi aḷittatu. tamiḻ ilakkiya kalaikkaḷañciyam, tamiḻ - cappāṉ - teluṅku - kaṉṉaṭam akaramutali ākiya paṇikaḷai inta niṟuvaṉam ceytuvarukiṟatu. itaṟkāka 1998-99-l urūpāy 20 ilakkam māṉiyamāka aracu aḷittatu. tamiḻmoḻi karuttaraṅku 
māvaṭṭan tōṟumuḷḷa aracu aluvalarkaḷukkum tamiḻ āṭcimoḻi āvatiluḷḷa niraikuṟaikaḷaic cuṭṭikkāṭṭumpaṭiyāṉa karuttaraṅkukaḷ naṭaipeṟṟaṉa. kuṟippāka aracāṇaikaḷ, caṭṭaṅkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟiṉ poruḷ viḷaṅkumpaṭiyum, avaṟṟai naṭaimuṟaip paṭuttum vakai paṟṟiyum karuttaraṅkil āyappaṭṭaṉa. ik karuttaraṅku kaḷāl 100viḻukkāṭu toyviṉṟit tamiḻ āṭcimoḻiyāṉatu. icaip paḷḷikaḷ 
ceṉṉai, maturai, kōvai ākiya iṭaṅkaḷil maṭṭumirunta icaip paḷḷikaḷaip pōlavē mēlum pattu māvaṭṭat talainakaraṅ kaḷil 1996-kkup piṉ icaip paḷḷikaḷ ēṟpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. ivaṟṟil mottam icai vallunarkaḷ paṇiyilamarttam ceyyap peṟṟaṉar. iṇaiyam (Internet) 
iṉṟaiya aṟiviyalil pūtta malarākat tikaḻvatu iṇaiyam. ulakattai oru noṭiyil iṇaikkum ikkaruviyiṉ payaṉai 1997-98ām āṇṭu tamiḻaka caṭṭamaṉṟa nikaḻcciyil naṭanta varavu - celavut tiṭṭa nikaḻcciyāl ulakam aṟintatu. virivupaṭuttappeṟṟa iṇaiyattāl tamiḻaka varalāṟu, tamiḻ ilakkiya varalāṟu, tamiḻk kalaikaḷiṉ ciṟappu, cuṟṟulā payaṇa vaḻikāṭṭutal ākiyavai ceytip pakkam"(Home Page), eṉum muṟaiyil tamiḻilum, āṅkilattilum tarappaṭukiṟatu. 
mēlum, iṇaiyattil ulakattilēyē atika ceytip pakkaṅ kaḷaik koṇṭa tamiḻiltāṉ mutalil veḷiyiṭap peṟṟatu. 1900 - l ulakattamiḻ iṇaiyam mānāṭu' tamiḻaka aracāl naṭattappeṟṟu ulakattamiḻ iṇaiyap palkalaikkaḻakam amaikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. intiya āṭcip paṇittērvu[I.A.S. Examination)

a.i.a.ti.mu.ka. āṭciyiṉ cātaṉaikaḷaic curuṅkak kāṇpōm. 1. piṟpaṭṭōr nalam mēmpaṭutal 
*' paṟṟuka piṟpaṭṭōr mēmpāṭṭai " itu tirāviṭa iyakkaṅ ḷiṉ cittāntam tamiḻnāṭṭil āṭciyaik kaippaṟṟa vēṇṭumāṉāl piṟpaṭuttappaṭṭa cātikaḷaic cikkaṉappiṭittukkoḷ" eṉṟa paṭṭāṅ kait ti.mu.ka.vukkup piṟaku a.i.a.ti.mu.ka.vum piṉpaṟṟi ataṉaiyē tērtalil tāraka mantiramāka uccarittup patavikku vantatāl a.iatimuka piṟpaṭṭōr nalattil akkaṟai koṇṭatu. a) vakuppu vārippaṭi nikarāḷiyam 
1921-l koṇṭuvarappaṭṭa vakuppu vārip paṭinikarāḷiyam 1951-l araciyal caṭṭa aṭippaṭai urimaikaḷaik kāṭṭi ucca nayaṉmai maṉṟattāl cellāteṉat taḷḷappaṭṭatu. piṉṉar ēṟpaṭṭa araciyal tiruttattil "piṟpaṭṭōr pātukāppup peṟṟaṉar. itaṉaṭippaṭaiyil tāṉ timuka āṭciyil 1969-l piṟpaṭṭōr nalattuṟaip paṭaikkap paṭṭatu. avarkaḷukkāka irunta iṭa otukkīṭu25liruntu 32 catavikitamāka uyarttappaṭṭatu. 
makōrā.(em.ji.ār āṭciyil poṟuppaiyēṟṟavuṭaṉ 1982 l cē.ē. ampācaṅkar āṇaikkuḻuvai amarttiṉār. atil 11 caṭṭamaṉṟa uṟuppiṉarkaḷum, piṟpaṭṭa nalattuṟai iyakkunar, caṭṭati tuṟait talaivar maṟṟum makkaḷ maṉṟat talaivarkaḷumāka 21 uṟuppi ṉarkaḷ iṭam peṟṟaṉar. piṉṉiṇaippu IV-l kāṇka) 
ik kuḻuviṉ parinturaiyiṉ pēril 1986-l piṟpaṭṭōr cātikaḷ 201 eṉa aṟiyappaṭṭatu. ivarkaḷ tamiḻaka makkaḷ tokaiyil 54 catavikitamāka uḷḷaṉareṉṟum kaṇakkiṭappaṭṭatu. ivarkaḷ kumukāya nilai, poruḷātāra nilai mutaliyavaṟṟil piṉtaṅkiyavarkaḷeṉṟum kūṟappaṭṭatu. eṉavē, piṟpaṭṭōrukku iṭa otukkīṭu ti.mu.ka. aracu ceyta 32 viḻukkāṭṭiliruntu 50 viḻukkāṭāka uyarntatu. inta otukkīṭu kalvi niṟuvaṉaṅkaḷilum, aracup paṇikaḷilum ceyalpaṭuttappaṭṭatu, ataṟkēṟṟāṟpōl varavu-celavil niti otukkīṭum uyarttappaṭṭatu.

kalapput tirumaṇam ceyvōrukkut taṅkappatakkam aḷikkum aṇṇāviṉ tiṭṭamum kaṭaippiṭikkappaṭṭatu, paḻaṅkuṭiyiṉariṉ mēmpāṭṭil akkaṟai kāṭṭappaṭṭatu. 3. kuḻantaikaḷ makaḷir nala mēmpāṭṭut tiṭṭaṅkaḷ 
peṇkaḷ muṉṉēṟṟattiṟkāka 'vaḻi kāṭṭum maiyaṅkaḷ" ceṉṉai, tañcai, maturai, kōvai ākiya nakaraṅkaḷil amaikkap paṭṭaṉa. ivaṟṟil ēḻai, ētili, ūṉamuṟṟa , vitavaip peṇkaḷukkāṉa

1983-l 'tamiḻnāṭup peṇkaḷ vaḷarccik kaḻakam" ēṟpaṭuttap paṭṭatu. vaḻikāṭṭum maiyaṅkaḷ mūlam mūṉṟu cēvai illaṅkaḷ" (Service Hormes) eṭṭut toḻil uṟpatti nilaiyaṅkaḷ ēṟpaṭuttap paṭṭaṉa. ivaṟṟil peṇkaḷukku piṉṉal, taiyal, taṭṭaccu mutaliya vaṟṟil payiṟci aḷikkappaṭṭatu. "makaḷir maṉṟaṅkaḷ" eṉavum toṭaṅ kappaṭṭaṉa. ittakaiya makaḷir maṉṟaṅkaḷ mottam II 220 iruntaṉa. ivaṟṟiṟkāka uruṭā12.5 ilakkam celaviṭappaṭṭatu. ivaṟṟil kuḻantai vaḷarppu, cattuṇavu, ciṟutoḻilkaḷ paṟṟip peṇkaḷukkuk kaṟpikkap paṭṭa ṉa. 
ivaṟṟait tavira kuḻantaikaḷ kāppakaṅkaḷ" tōrāyamāka 1988 nāṭu muḻuvatum ēṟpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. ivaṟṟil akavai mutal ikkuṭpaṭṭa kuḻantaikaḷ pēṇikkākkappaṭṭaṉar. itaṉāl kuḻantaikaḷ iṟappu vikitam kuṟaintatu. ittakaiya makaḷir kuḻantai nala niṟuvaṉaṅkaḷ ḷāl kuṟippāka vitavaikaḷ, ūṉamuṟṟap peṇkaḷ, kaiviṭappaṭṭap peṇkaḷ ākiyōrum aṉātaikaḷāṉa kuḻantaikaḷum kāppāṟṟap paṭṭaṉar. 1986-87l maṭṭum kuḻantaikaḷaip pēṇik kākka urupā 117 82 ilakkam celaviṭappaṭṭatu,

- ovvoru ūrum ''taṉṉiṟaivu '' peṟavēṇṭumeṉpatē makōrā. em.ji.ār) viṉ nōkkam. ataṉaṭippaṭaiyil ovvoru ūrilum pātukākkappaṭṭa kuṭinīr, nakaraṅkaḷōṭu iṇaikkum pātaikaḷ, pōkkuvarattu ēntukaḷ, pāvaṅkaḷ mutaliyaṉa niṟaivu ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. ellā ūrkaḷukkum miṉviḷakkum, tolaipēcit toṭarpum ēṟpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. āti - tirāviṭar kuṭiyiruppukaḷukku ivaṟṟil muṉṉurimai tarappaṭṭatu. avarkaḷukkuk kuṭinīr, iṭukāṭu, cuṭukāṭu, miṉviḷakku mutaliya ēntukaḷ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. maruttuva maṉaikaḷ, tāy-cēy nalaviṭutikaḷ mutaliyaṉavum ēṟpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. paḷḷikkūṭaṅkaḷ illāta ūrkaḷē illai. āti tirāviṭarkaḷukku

1977-78-l kalvi vaḷarccikkāka urupā 150 kōṭi celaviṭap paṭṭatu. 1985-86-l itu 534 kōṭiyāka uyarntatu. mēlnilaippaṭippu 
a.i.a.ti.mu.ka. āṭciyiṉ pōtu uyarnilaip paḷḷippaṭippu pattu āṇṭukaḷōṭu kārppurattilēyē mēl nilaikkalvi" iraṇṭu tuṇṭukaḷ paṭikka vaḻi ceyyappaṭṭatu. itaṉāl pattāṇṭukaḷ paṭittu viṭṭuk kallūriyil paṭikka nakarppuram cellum vaḻakkam māṟi, karp purattilēyē kallūrip paṭippu vantuviṭṭatu. aṭuttu, kallūri iḷaṅkalaip paṭippu mūṉṟāṇṭāka māṟiyatu. āṉāl mēlnilaip paṭippu muṭinta uṭaṉēyē maruttuvam, poṟiyiyal mutaliya toḻiṟ paṭippukaḷai ūriliruntē nērē ceṉṟu paṭikkum vāyppu ēṟpaṭṭatu. eṉavē, ūrilēyē maruttuvar, poṟiyāḷar, aṟiviyalār, toḻil tuṟai vallunar, mutaliyōrai mēlnilaip paḷḷippaṭippu nirṇayittu viṭṭatu. palkalaikkaḻakaṅkaḷ 
. kōvaiyil pāratiyār palkalaikkaḻakamum, tirucciyil pārati tācaṉ palkalaikkaḻakamum, tañcaiyil tamiḻp palkalaikkaḻakamum, ceṉṉaiyil toḻil nuṭpap palkalaikkaḻakamum, kōṭaikkāṉalil aṉṉai terēcā palkalaikkaḻakamum, kāraikkuṭiyil aḻakappā palkalaikkaḻakamum, toṭaṅkappaṭṭaṉa.

paḷḷikaḷ aracup paḷḷikaḷ, uḷḷāṭcip paḷḷikaḷ eṉa iruvakaiyāka iruntatai ivvaracu oṭṭumottamāka ellāvaṟṟaiyum * 'aracuppaḷḷikaḷ" eṉṟākkiyatu. itaṉāl āciriyarkaḷ aṉaivarum aracu ūḻiyarkaḷāyiṉar. avarkaḷiṉ eṇṇikkaiyum, pātukāppum atikarittaṉa. paṇimuṟaikaḷ, ōyvu ūtiyam mutaliyaṉa orē cīrāka amaintaṉa. iruvakaik kallūrikaḷ 
kallūrikaḷum aracuk kallūrikaḷ, taṉiyār kallūrikaḷeṉa iru vakaiyākac ceyalpaṭṭaṉa. avaṟṟil paṇiyāṟṟum āciriyarkaḷiṉ nilaikaḷilum, paṇi, ūtiyam mutaliyavaṟṟilum vēṟupāṭukaḷiruntaṉa. ivai palkalaikkaḻaka māṉiyakkuḻuviṉ parinturaippaṭi orē cīrāṉavai yāka ākkappaṭṭaṉa. aracukkallūrikaḷilum, taṉiyār kallūrikaḷilum paṇiyāṟṟum āciriyarkaḷukku orēvitamāṉa ūtiyam vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatu. itaiyum aracē nēraṭiyāka vaḻaṅkiyatu. aṉatiya vikitam, paṇi nilaikaḷ mutaliyaṉavum ōyvu ūtiyamum ivarkaḷukku orē mātiriyāka nirṇa yikkappaṭṭaṉa, toḻil nuṭpak kallūrikaḷ, vēḷāṇmaik kal lūrikaḷ, poṟi iyal kallūrikaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟil paṇiyāṟṟum āciri yarkaḷum itē nilaiyil matikkappaṭṭaṉar. itaṉāl, māntavaṉamum āṟṟalum vaḷarntaṉa, 8, nalvāḻvu mēmpāṭu (a) cukātāra vaḷarcci 
ūrppuraṅkaḷil nalavāḻvai mēmpaṭuttac cuṟṟuc cūḻalkaḷait tūymaippaṭuttutal, toṟṟunōykaḷ paravāmal taṭuttal, taramāṉa maruntu māttiraikaḷaik koṭuttal, nōy taṭuppu muṟaikaḷaik kaiyāḷutal mutaliyavaṟṟai aracu ceytatu.kuṟippākattāy, cēy nalattai mukāmaiyācukkoṇṭatu. vātanōy taṭuppu taṭuppuc coṭṭu maruntu viṭutal, ammai, mañcaḷkāmālait taṭuppūci, cattuṇavu vaḻaṅkutal mutaliyavaṟṟai mēṟkoṇṭatu. 
putiya maruttuvamaṉaikaḷ, maruttuvak kalluārikaḷ mutaliyaṉa ēṟpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. eṭṭu maruttuvak kalluārikaḷai amaittu, āṇṭu tōṟum 1,015 maruttuvarkaḷai uruvākki, makkaḷukkuppaṇipuriya aṉuppappaṭṭaṉar. mottattil 14 māvaṭṭa maruttuvamaṉaikaḷ, 104 vaṭṭa maruttuvamaṉaikaḷ, avarkaḷil toṭakkac cukātāra maruttuva nilaiyaṅkaḷ ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. puṟṟunōy taṭuppu, kuṭumpak kaṭṭuppāṭu 
ākiyavai muṭukkamāy mēṟkoḷḷappaṭṭaṉa.

9. miṉuṟpattiyil mēmpāṭu - 1970-71l 57 mekāvāṭ yūṉiṭṭukaḷāyirunta miṉuṟpatti 1985 - 86-l paṟkaḷ mekāvāṭ yūṉiṭṭukaḷāy uyarttappaṭṭatu. miṉ uṟpatti nilakkariyai erittum, aṇuvaip payaṉpaṭuttiyum peṟappaṭṭatu. vēṇṭiya nilakkariyai iṟakkumati ceytu miṉuṟpatti ceyyappaṭṭatu. kalpākkattil aṇumiṉ uṟpatti ceyyappaṭṭatu. pōtiya nilakkari tamiḻnāṭṭil kiṭaikkātatāl 1985-86-l urupā 57.87 kōṭi miṉ uṟpattikkākac celaviṭappaṭṭatu. 
putitāka ūrppuraṅkaḷukkum, kuṭicaippakutikaḷukkum miṉcāram vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatāl 1986-l maṭṭum 45ilakkam kuṭicaikaḷil miṉviḷak kerintatu. āti - tirāviṭar kuṭiyiruppukaḷ mottam 64,012kku miṉ viḷakkukaḷ ilavacamāka aḷikkappaṭṭaṉa. 1984-85-l 074 ilakkam vēḷāṇmainīriraippu iyantiraṅkaḷukku māṉiya vilaiyil miṉcāram vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatu. itaṉāl, vēḷāṇmai uṟpatti atikarittatu. 10, pōkkuvarattu ēntukaḷ mēmpaṭutal 
1985-l tamiḻaka ūrkaḷiṉ makkaḷ tokai 324 kōṭiyākum. ūrppañcāyattukkaḷiṉ eṇṇikkai 12,339 ākum, nakaraṅkaḷiṉ eṇṇikkai 434 ākum. 1985-l tamiḻakattil ōṭiya pēruntukaḷiṉ eṇ kaṇikkai 15,021 ākum. ivaṟṟil 12 pōkkuvarattukkaḻakaṅkaḷ 7,829 pēruntukaḷai iyakkiṉa. ōvvoru nāḷum 56 ilakkam payaṇikaḷ pēruntukaḷil payaṇittaṉar. nāḷtōṟum 35 ilakkam kilō mīṭṭar tārampēruntukaḷ ōṭiṉa. 
pēruntukaḷai nāṭṭuṭamaiyākkiya aṇṇāviṉ kālantoṭṭu, tamiḻnāṭṭup pōkkuvarattukkaḻakam palkip perukiyatu. kuṟippāka mūlai muṭukkukaḷiluḷḷa ūrkaḷukkellām pēruntukaḷ ceṉṟaṉa. itaṉāl 440 ūrkaḷ pēruntup pōkkuvarattāl iṇaikkappaṭṭaṉa, itaṉāl a.i.a.ti.mu.ka. aracu 400 putiya pēruntukaḷai 163 kōṭi urupā celavil vāṅkiyatu. iṉṟu intiya mānilaṅkaḷilēyē 24 maṇi nēramum eṇticaikaḷilum nillāmal ōṭikkoṇṭirukkum pēruntup pōkkuvarattu uḷḷa mānilam tamiḻakam maṭṭumēyām. 11. nīrppācaṉa mēmpāṭukaḷ 
nīrppācaṉaṅkaḷai mutaltaram, iraṇṭām taram, ciṟunīrppācaṉam eṉa mūṉṟākap pirittup paṭippaṭiyākavum, taṉit taṉiyākavum, avaṟṟiṉ tarattai aracu mēmpaṭuttiyatu. 1985-il maṭṭum mutaltara iraṇṭāmtara nīrppācaṉaṅkaḷukkāka aracu 48.12 kōṭi urupācelaviṭṭatu. ciṟunīrp pācaṉa mēmpāṭṭiṟkāka urupā 4.62 kōṭi celaviṭṭatu. pañca nivāraṇam

āṅkilēyarkaḷāl ēṟpaṭuttappaṭṭa ik kaḻakam kumukāyap poruḷātāra vaḷarccikkut taṭaiyāka iruntatu. itaṉāl 1.12.1980-l makōrā em ji āri oḻittār. 2. pārampariya marmaṇiyakkārar, karṇam oḻippu 
kōyililē arccakar pataviyaippōl ip patavikaḷum takappa ṉukkuppiṉ makaṉ yeṉṟa muṟaiyil paramparaic cottāka iruntaṉa, itaṉāl kuṟippiṭṭa cātiyiṉar paramparaiyāka otukkappaṭṭu, oṭukkap paṭṭaṉar. inta paramparai muṟaiyai oḻittu, avaṟṟaiyum aracuppaṇika ḷākka vēṇṭumeṉa āti tirāviṭarkaḷiṉ talaivarkaḷ paṉṉeṭuṅ kālamākak kēṭṭu vantaṉar. āṉāl, itu makōrā, emjiār) kālattil tāṉ niṟaivēṟṟappaṭṭatu. paramparaip patavi muṟaiyai oḻittu itaṉai ūr aluvalar (Village Administrative Officials) eṉa māṟṟiṉār, tērvāṇaiyam naṭattum tērvil tēṟiya evarum ip paṇiyil amarttappaṭṭaṉar. mēlum, aracuppaṇikaḷ vakuppuvāri paṭinikarāḷiyat tiṉ aṭippaṭaiyil amarttappaṭuvatāl ūr maṇiyakkārarākavum, karṇamāka vum ōr āti tirāviṭarkūṭa varalāmeṉṟa niyati ēṟpaṭṭu viṭṭatu. intiya āṭcippaṇi, intiyak kāvalpaṇi (I.A.S & I.P.S) ākiyavaṟṟil tēṟiya āti tirāviṭar māvaṭṭa āṭciyāḷarākavum, māvaṭṭak kāvaltuṟai atikāriyākavum vantapōtum ūr maṇiyakkāra rāka varamuṭiyāta nilai iruntatai makōrā. em.ji.ār māṟṟiyamait tār. itu kumukāyattilēṟpaṭṭa māperum puraṭci ''aracu āti - tirāvi ṭarkaḷukkuppōṭum nalattiṭṭaṅkaḷ avarkaḷaip pōy aṭaiyāmal taṭuttavarkaḷ paramparai maṇiyakkārarum, karṇamumēyāvar; iṉi attakaiya taṭai ēṟpaṭātu" eṉṟu. ivvāṇaiyai veḷiyiṭumpōtu makōrā. em ji ār kūṟiṉār. 3. uḷḷāṭcit tuṟaikaḷil iṭa otukkīṭu
